# [2026-08-11] arc+closeout | HARNESS-01 COMPLETE on `main` (PR #902, closes the #119 scope) — the layered TrAIder harness: soul, contract, doctrine, manuals, lessons, and the Lab that measures it

**The dev fleet's context-corpus discipline, given to trading minds.** Design ratified in #871/#890 (HN-D1..D5 all A); six slices built → adversarially reviewed → folded → merged on `feature/harness-01`; arc→main PR #902 merged by Graham 2026-08-11. Arc-tip gate EXIT=0 (api 2054 · shared 958 · agent 646 · web 1772).

A harnessed mind (seed `pnpm --filter @traidit/api seed:platform-harness` + manifest `mindBinding.harnessRef: "platform"`, a versioned checkpoint act) wakes with: **soul** (S2 — user-editable persona/mandate/disposition, seeded explicitly from mission with the act in the rationale, riding the context payload); **the operating contract** (S2 — authority boundary / gate subordination / honesty doctrine / falsifier duty, verified true sentence-by-sentence against the platform's machinery, riding a fleet-cached second system block); **the tool doctrine** (S3 — 7 family briefs + 52 cards GENERATED from the quant contract so doctrine cannot drift from code, wisdom honest-absent, cards load only for cited tools); **the task manuals** (S4 — exemplars deduped from the template to the layer-4 shelf; `trader-planner@1` sha256-byte-frozen for unharnessed agents; `@2` fixes the standing "the gate still clamps" falsehood — the gate BLOCKS — and renders only when its own promises are true: honesty-gated on the shelf actually riding); **the guarded lessons** (S5 — a lesson that cannot cite ledger evidence is a story and is REFUSED as shape; scenario-fork revisions ruled un-citable: a replayed counterfactual is a hypothesis wearing experience's clothes). Every authored artifact stamps `harnessVersion`. **Every agent without a harnessRef is byte-identical to today — proven at every slice, not asserted.**

**Safety by construction (HN-D1-A):** platform layers have NO write route — a user cannot edit gate-obedience out of their TrAIder because no endpoint would accept the attempt (route/store/seed/migration/import censuses pinned). **The Lab (S6, HN-D5-A):** two-arm A/B on the lab session (never a dial — the shadow-recipe discipline), comparison = a MEASUREMENT PASS (verdicts recorded never applied, preserving arm-input equality), pinned-revision binding, economics honest (null never a fabricated $0); **promote records the evaluated decision append-only and flips nothing** — no live hot-swap exists. Migrations 045/046.

**The review culture's tally:** every slice review caught something real pre-merge — a stale-seed silent degradation (fixed by honesty-gating), a doctrine-paraphrase drift surface, an unwritten regex ruling, and S6's B1: a stale-tips check whose extra strictness made the mandated old-vs-new promote STRUCTURALLY IMPOSSIBLE across 2,053 green tests — found only by a reviewer walking the flow. **Named notes:** the shared block is effectively full (12,171B vs the 12,288B pin — future growth = cuts or a Graham ruling, never a silently raised pin); open residuals written in code where the next slice's reviewer will find them (lesson evidence-ref EXISTENCE verification unbuilt — follow-up ruling; mind-authored retrospectives — later arc; the sessionCraft teaching line — itself a harness change the Lab should A/B; scenario-fork lane deliberately unharnessed). Record: `docs/design/v2/agent-architecture/harness-01-design-packet/`.

# [2026-08-11] arc+closeout | RUNWAY-CLOSE COMPLETE on `main` (PR #895) — #874 forward run-summary read + PORTFOLIO-READ-01 Slice 2 the recorded equity curve (migration 043)

**Two UI-runway backend gaps filled on `main` 2026-08-11 via integration PR #895** (merged to main at 2026-08-11T16:09:44Z; slices PR #891 #874 merged 2026-08-11T14:35:26Z + PR #894 #875 S2 merged 2026-08-11T14:41:50Z; PR #887 #875 S1 landed separately 2026-08-11T14:09:29Z). Migration ceiling: **043**. Gate at tip: `bash scripts/local-ci.sh` → **OVERALL: PASS**, true exit 0.

- **#874 — forward run-summary read** (`GET /forward/runs/:runId/summary`, PR #891). The `agent_runs.run_summary_json` written by the forward lane (migration 040) now has a dedicated read path — cadence, terminal state, requisition disposition, and the FLIGHT-RECORDER `distanceToTrigger` are now readable. **Endpoint choice rationale (from PR #895):** chose a dedicated per-run read over enriching `/status` because a forward session persists N runs (one per traded symbol) at one instant, so hanging one summary off `latestSession` would publish an arbitrary symbol's accounting as "the session's" (uuid tie-break). Three distinct answers: **400** malformed id · **404** no such forward run · **200 + `summary: null`** honest absence. Malformed stored document throws loudly rather than degrading into absence — corruption and absence are different facts. No migration.

- **#875 Slice 2 — the recorded equity curve** (`GET /portfolio/equity?scope=agent|deployment`, PR #894, **migration 043**). Append-only equity-snapshot store + the ruled snapshot-header/series-body read. **The ruling's core rule enforced structurally:** the series is a discriminated union whose `empty` branch **has no points field** — a fabricated curve cannot survive the type, let alone the parse. `on conflict do nothing` with no update or delete method: a record of what was observed, never a mutable projection. Degraded points read back degraded (`completeness`/`truthPosture`/`sourcePosture` stored with the money). **Migration 043 fence:** surveyed across every remote head — claimed by this arc alone; ceiling was 042 (live-deployment took 041/042).

**Honesty properties of the endpoints:** (1) per-run read trichotomy (400/404/null) with malformed-throws-loudly keeps corruption distinct from absence; (2) equity series empty branch having no points field means a fabricated curve cannot survive the type, so the UI cannot render a lie; (3) both endpoints rest on append-only storage with no update/delete methods, making them records of what was observed rather than mutable projections.

**Verification:** Gate on the integrated tip: local-ci OVERALL: PASS (install · migrations · corpus:validate · corpus:current · lint · typecheck · shared · agent · api · web · build:web), true exit 0. Real-PG proof (#875 S2, throwaway scratch DB, dropped): 043 applies clean + idempotently; an overwrite attempt at an existing `(scope, asOf)` returned 0 rows and the original `missing_price` point survived; out-of-order inserts read back ascending with sub-cent precision intact; CHECKs refused malformed money and posture. Sentinel probes red across both slices, incl. compile-time (dropping `distanceToTrigger` breaks the build via a contract bridge).

**Follow-ups minted, not silently absorbed:** #892 (session-scoped run fan-out — only one run id is reachable today). #875 remains OPEN: the remaining follow-up is the recorder cadence + the FE render contract (#576). #874 now CLOSED.

# [2026-08-10] arc+milestone | LIVE-DEPLOY-01 (#745) BUILT — the ladder's third act: real money as three explicit founder acts, guarded twice, recorded honestly, executable only by ritual

The switch FT-D8 left *named and unpulled* is pulled — as machinery, never as an event. The full promote is three explicit artifacts: a checkpoint declaring `environmentMode: "live"` (LD-D6's teeth — the gate reads the MANIFEST via `AgentLiveDeclarationGate`, never the mutable `lab_agents.environment_mode` row; row-live+manifest-lab refuses 409 with no row and no event) → a live-environment deployment (migration **041**: the axis, immutable per row, born `dry_run` on both environments) → the submit flip. Five slices, PRs #878/#880/#881/#882/#883; migrations **041/042**; register **LD-D1..D6 (Graham) + LD-B1..B8 (build-time) + C1..C3 + FU-1..FU-5**.

**Guarded twice, weakened never.** Trestle's binding-site trace proved the ruled "add an environment parameter to a byte-unchanged guard" literally unsatisfiable (the paper pin is a hard equality asserted at adapter CONSTRUCTION — even the live dry-run rung was blocked): resolution LD-B1 = the paper guard byte-identical and still authoritative for paper, a new equally-strict **sibling live guard** (`api.alpaca.markets` hard pin), and an exhaustive dispatcher keyed on the row's immutable environment whose `never`-typed default THROWS. Two refusals now exist that never did: a live URL on a paper row, a paper URL on a live row. The live client's credentials are a separate env set (`ALPACA_LIVE_*`), absent-creds is a typed failure naming the SET, and until LD-S5 a real-money order was **impossible by absence of a method** — the strongest intermediate state available.

**The seams were where the defects lived — both caught by the slice-review rhythm, neither by a test.** LD-S4-F1: every slice individually honest, yet a live `submit` fail-closed at the last unwired connection — it would have been discovered at the machine, credentials loaded, mid-ritual (→ LD-B6, slice S5). LD-S5-F1: a live submit DEP would have stamped `environment: "paper"` / `hasLiveAuthority: false` under a real-money order — order facts real, labels lying, in the record everything downstream learns from (→ LD-B7: environment stamps from the row with `?? "paper"` so an omission can only UNDER-claim; a live dry-run honestly stamps "live"+all-false — *meant real money, authorized to move none*; paper DEPs frozen whole-object byte-identical). `hasTruePnl` stays false on every arm (FU-5): the flag claims P&L truth-AVAILABILITY and no live P&L surface exists — flipping it on the mere existence of real fills would be the overclaim.

**Proof posture.** The real-DB debt paid on a live API boot: schema 40→42, the running worker never restarted, deployment `845884d2` backfilled `paper` with every field identical; `agent_lifecycle_events` empty — paper deploys mint NO row, because promotion IS the lab→live boundary. The scratch-DB chain proof runs inside the gate. The fixture census: zero, everywhere, per the pre-authorization's delete-before-merge expiry. `supervised_submit_ritual_v0.md` is cold-executable with FU-4's re-verification as flip-checklist line 1 and LD-B5's undeploy-does-not-flatten in the abort reads. **Honestly unproven and deliberately so:** the real `api.alpaca.markets` account read (no live credentials on the operator machine — ritual §3.1) and the first real-money order, which is Graham's supervised act and no one else's.

# [2026-08-10] build | POLYGON-EXODUS-02 · PX-S5a — the gateway `TickBarService` seam (PX-D3 = A2-with-seam), plus the S0-follow-up findings that reshape the quant half

**The seam is extracted and the scenario source rides it — byte-identically.** `TickBarService` (`apps/api/src/services/provider-integrations/tick-bar.service.ts`) is the one named gateway module that owns "Alpaca raw tick tape → policy-versioned 1-second bars for a single session" on the EXISTING `aggregateAlpacaTradePages` + `@traidit/integrations-alpaca` trades client. Its `buildSessionBars` carries every AHL honesty guarantee verbatim — no fabricated bar, no truncated tape sold as a session, the AHL-S3 tri-state adjustment basis (refuse rather than downgrade), the auction-minute `volumeSuspect` estimate flag (AHL-D1), the multi-day/malformed-window local refusals, the SIP-only feed — and answers in a provider-neutral `TickBarResult` (`served` / `refused` with a typed `unavailableCode`) that any consumer translates into its own contract. `AlpacaScenarioBarSource.fetchFromTicks` collapses from ~120 lines to a thin translator (scenario source **−201/+35**); its 25-test spec is the regression harness and stays green unedited. A new `tick-bar.service.spec.ts` (9 tests) pins the seam's OWN contract — the discriminated union a future quant consumer will depend on. **Gate:** api typecheck (src + spec) clean · lint clean · **api suite 1682/1682**.

**The S0 probe's quant-half assumptions did not survive reconnaissance — the interception is HELD for a ruling.** Mapping the gateway→quant surface turned up three facts the packet's PX-S5a text predates: (1) **there is no `edge-score` endpoint** anywhere — it is compute + batch only; (2) on the REST compute/batch lane `interval:"1s"` lives ONLY nested in a `WindowSpec`, and **no internal lane ever emits it** (LAB is fixed `1d`, SIM sends caller-supplied bars) — so the "swap a 1s window for caller bars" interception guards a path nothing internal drives, and its per-symbol tick-stream cost (~31 s/symbol-day) makes it viable only for the single-session shape; (3) the ACTUALLY-reachable 1s consumer is the **SSE stream lane** (`/stream/compute`, the "one UI flip from live" algo-dashboard), whose `StreamComputeRequest` contract **has no `bars` field at all** — caller-supplied bars are not expressible there without a contract change. Quant-side confirms the tie is the **1s REST window** (`alpaca_rest.py` has no 1s → routes to `PolygonAdapter`), while live 1s *streaming* is already an Alpaca observer (`alpaca.py`). So "gateway intercepts a 1s window → supplies bars" as written applies to a lane nothing internally exercises, and PX-S5b's "remove 1s, Polygon serves nothing" needs the stream-lane question ruled first. The seam (needed under every resolution) ships now; the quant-facing wiring waits on a decision.

PR #865 merged (`99bccf39`; #844 closed) — the trio's missing leg. Forward runs now persist the SAME `RunSummary` vocabulary SIM does (`agent_runs.run_summary_json`, migration **040**, written in the run's own transaction — the summary IS the run's record), carrying FLIGHT-RECORDER's distance-to-trigger computed over the ONE frame selection shared with the shadow sweep (`deriveForwardShadowEvaluation` now *takes* the selection — sharing enforced by the type, so distances, shadows, and the frames row can never disagree about which bars a run walked).

**The Graham-ruled fold-in: exact synthetic-tick recovery (FS-D4).** The 08-04/05 live census showed the open-minutes MAJORITY of forward runs losing their entire diagnostic bank to SHADOW-PLANS-02's conservative `SYNTHETIC_FRESHNESS_TICK` refusal — Tuesday banked zero sweeps, 8/8 refused. The mechanics census (the fenced host, read-only) proved the refusal stricter than the truth: the tick is SINGULAR and TERMINAL by construction (the in-loop probe `break`s immediately; the trailing probe is guarded by `plan.status !== "broken"` while the tick is guaranteed-to-break; F6 floors ≥1 real bar before any tick). So a dead-feed run's real frames are EXACTLY the leading `barsEvaluated − 1` due entries — arithmetic, not reconstruction — with two guards that degrade any future invariant break to the existing NAMED refusals rather than a guess. `EVALUATIONS_SUPPRESSED` stays a refusal (a suppression hole's position is genuinely unknowable). Corrections log C1: the driver had been dropping `observedBarsSeen`/`evaluationPolicy` the agnostic host always returned (2-line pass-through — deriving from the offered array would have fabricated a count); the guaranteed-breaking tick's dependency on the linter-mandatory `dataFreshness` assertion is now written down, with a named two-tick residual flagged-not-built.

**The census rider answered a standing question with code + SQL:** the prediction-resolution pass FIRES correctly and 0 resolutions is the CORRECT state — all 4 live filings carry `resolveBy 2026-09-12`, all correctly decide `open`, and the open arm writes nothing. Two genuine findings minted, not silently absorbed: **#863** (resolution is gated behind the intraday universe bar-book build though it needs only daily closes — a no-priceable day settles nothing) and **#864** (an all-open pass logs nothing, operationally indistinguishable from never running).

**Verified:** ratchet byte-empty (evaluator, the whole `game-script-host/` dir, the Phase-1 shadow pure lane, DEP schema, goldens) · three sentinel probes red→revert→green (dropped summary write, off-by-one recovery, distances-despite-refusal) · **real-DB proof `FS_PROOF_OK`** — migration 040 applied live, the previously-refused dead-feed shape recovered its 6 real frames and the full chain banked off them (frames + sweep + read seam served under the SP-D8 keystone), the summary with 3 distance records persisted and read back on a real `agent_runs` row, suppression unchanged. Worker rebooted onto `99bccf39` — from the next session, every forward run banks summary + distances, and the recovered majority banks its shadows too.

# [2026-08-03] arc | SHADOW-PLANS-02 COMPLETE — the forward lane gets out-of-sample counterfactuals; the frame tap that was wrong AND unnecessary

PR #843 merged (`cbdbde5f`; #842 closed) — designed, built, proven, and landed the same day, discharging the Phase-2 slice SHADOW-PLANS-01 deferred (SP-D3). The frozen 12-variant sweep now derives beside every FORWARD session: the first counterfactuals computed on tape the mind had never seen at authoring. Composed with FLIGHT-RECORDER-01 (merged the same morning), every forward session banks the R&D trio — what the mind saw (packets), how close its rules came (distances, SIM-persisted; forward home = #844), what its neighbours would have done (shadows).

**The design was corrected by the build, and the corrections are the story (packet corrections log C1).** The ruling proposed a pass-through tap on the frame source. Marlow proved both halves false against the code: (1) dueness is decided INSIDE the host after the source yields, so a source tap records OBSERVED frames, never the due subset — a sweep over them would perturb `requisition`, the exact SP-P1 violation Phase 1's C1.1 discipline exists to prevent; (2) the tap was unnecessary — the forward lane hands `runLivePaperHost` a MATERIALIZED array the caller already holds. What shipped instead: `forward-evaluated-frames.ts` recovers the evaluated set as the leading `barsEvaluated` entries of the due subsequence (the invariant stated checkably against the host's own loop), with four NAMED refusals (`NO_EVALUATED_BARS` / `EVALUATIONS_SUPPRESSED` / `SYNTHETIC_FRESHNESS_TICK` / `CADENCE_SELECTION_DIVERGED`) — absent-with-reason, never a guessed bar set — reusing the SIM lane's own `selectDueBarFrames` so the two lanes cannot disagree silently. Net: no decorator, and `runGameScriptHost`, the live-paper host and adapter all BYTE-UNTOUCHED — stronger than the ruling's own promise. A third correction killed the FR-digest cross-check (the packet's sha covers the authoring book; this table holds evaluated frames — equality would manufacture integrity findings out of correct data).

**Persistence with the keystone.** Migration **037** `forward_run_frames` stores the evaluated due frames verbatim — correct where ADR-006 forbade SIM persistence, because a live session's frames are non-regenerable. That single bounded table (≈20 rows of bars per symbol-day) makes the SP-D8 keystone hold for forward rows identically to SIM. The capture is CONTAINED (SP-P5/FR-D2): frames-first-sweep-second (a failed frame capture SKIPS the sweep rather than storing a recipe nothing can re-derive), its own transaction, never the run's; failures resolve to `SHADOW_CAPTURE_ABSENT`/`SHADOW_CAPTURE_FAILED` and the session completes normally. The read seam learned forward resolution; the SIM path is unchanged; the 027 repositories took zero edits.

**Verified:** ratchet byte-empty (plan-evaluator, the WHOLE game-script-host dir, the Phase-1 pure lane, DEP schema, goldens) at every slice + an independent orchestrator gate re-run · three sentinel probes red→revert→green · hand-oracled expected values passed first try · **REAL-DB proof `SP2_PROOF_OK`** — 037 applied on a database already at 036, 30 frames + 1 recipe + 8 variant summaries persisted through the production repositories, and the production `ShadowReadService.readByRun` re-derived and SERVED the forward run under the keystone. The proof also tripped the keystone's negative arm live: with no armed revision persisted it refused `missing-revision` rather than serving — the honesty floor demonstrating itself unprompted.

**Follow-ups:** #844 (forward runs persist no run summary — forward distances homeless) · dead-feed sessions bank no sweep by conservative refusal (needs a ruling only if it proves common). The armed forward deployment's worker was rebooted onto `cbdbde5f` — the next session is the first to bank packets + shadows + frames together.

# [2026-08-03] arc | FLIGHT-RECORDER-01 COMPLETE — every authoring session is evaluation-grade now: what the mind SAW, how close its rules CAME, how long its calls TOOK

PR #828 merged (`cc4fdc35`; #771/#772 closed; **#770 stays open** for Phase-2 mind-emitted attribution). The census gap it closes: three weeks of live sessions produced outcomes with no evaluation substrate — the 200-bar lookback the mind authored from vanished at session end, a no-fire day could not say how close a rule came, and authoring latency was unmeasured.

**FR-D1 — the evidence packet.** `authoring_evidence_packets` (migration 033), keyed by the **existing** per-revision `evidencePacketRef` — no new identity. Packet = E1 spot + window anchors + a **bar digest** (count/firstTs/lastTs/ohlcvSha256 over the canonical sequence — identity at ~100 B, not ~200 stored bars) + each granted tool's rendered outputs **verbatim** off the composed book (reference-identity-tested: the packet points at the very object the prompt rendered, so recorded values cannot drift from what the mind read). Captured at all three ref-mint sites: forward authoring, SIM authoring **including the intraday re-plan**, campaign intake. **FR-D2** — capture is CONTAINED (the SP-P5 posture) and deliberately outside the run transaction: failure → logged `EVIDENCE_CAPTURE_FAILED`, session completes; proven by sentinel (throwing DB *and* malformed book both resolve and name the loss). **FR-D3** — distance-to-trigger is a pure post-pass beside the evaluator walking the run's own due frames (C1.1): price-level kinds measured exact-decimal (`"0"` iff fired; first-on-ties closest bar), non-price kinds honestly absent — never a fabricated zero; rides `run_summary_json` additively in both lanes. **FR-D4** — wall-clock via an injectable clock: the plan lane **sums** across a session's attempts (one row per session-source), the intake records **per invocation** (one row per call); `MindTrace.latencyMs` → `agent_token_economics.latency_ms`, historical rows NULL.

**The ratchet never moved** — evaluator, gate, host wiring, DEP schema, all 34 goldens byte-identical to `main`, re-asserted after every one of THREE catch-up merges (the arc absorbed CAMPAIGN-02 #815 and MINDCONFIG-01 #837 mid-flight; the only real conflicts were both-sides-appended constructor tails and the both-sides-appended MindTrace field). **Live-proven on the real DB three times**, the last two against a **deliberately cleared packet table** so row existence proved the shipped tip wrote them: exact-ref packets with digests, 3 measured distance records on the rules-bearing ladder fixture, `latency_ms` flowing; migration 033 slotted cleanly into a database already at 034/035. First PR through the re-instated Actions CI — 10/10 checks green.

**Build-time corrections, logged not laundered:** the register's `unsupported-condition-kind` status was never reachable (metric-only rules resolve to the clearer `no-price-condition`) — the type now advertises only the two absences the pass actually produces. The proof exercise independently re-confirmed **#773** twice (SIM authoring rows are stamped scenario-time; wall-clock "recent" filters silently find nothing — query by session). Operational lesson for /tmp worktrees: macOS reaped a worktree's git metadata overnight mid-arc — zero loss because every meaningful commit had been pushed; **push after every meaningful commit in /tmp worktrees** is now doctrine-grade habit.

**Why it matters for R&D:** from `cc4fdc35` forward, every forward/SIM/intake authoring accrues its evidence packet, per-rule closest-approach, and mind latency automatically — the raw material for claim-vs-packet verifiers (#770 Ph2), authoring-faithfulness scoring, and the EvalCal instrument surface, accumulating from day one instead of being reconstructed later.
# [2026-08-03] merge+milestone | MINDCONFIG-01 (EP-37 #698) COMPLETE on `main` — PR #837: model choice as agent identity, house-key Anthropic, Settings tab real, Mind field in creation

The founder-phase pull-forward lands: **a MindConfiguration is a named, persisted entity** (provider + model + params, never key material — the Credential attach point recorded in migration 036's comment, not built; house-key Anthropic only this arc) **selectable in agent creation and editable in the Settings window's LLM Configuration tab** (the ruled ST-1 placeholder is gone). Manifests carry a **`mindConfigRef`**, never embedded provider/model; identity by ref, truth by stamp (the per-run mind-binding record resolves ref → config at assembly, recording the resolved provider+model + config `updated_at`; a missing/archived config **fail-stops** the run with MIND_UNAVAILABLE). **The model catalog is in-repo, versioned, and rate-cards are mandatory** (the #758 law made mechanical — a build-time check rejects any entry without a card). **The authoring cost estimate is measured × rate card, labeled an estimate** (EP-32's observed token distributions × the entry's rate card; per-agent actuals replace estimates once the agent runs under that config). **Amendment MC-D5-A (RULED same-day):** when the measured distribution's tokenizer family differs from the selected model's, the estimate surfaces as a RANGE (raw × card to raw × card × 1.3, the vendor-published skew factor), with the reason legible — same-family estimates stay a plain labeled estimate.

# [2026-08-03] ruling | PX-D3 RULED = A2-WITH-SEAM; PX-D9 (tape-service fanout) adopted as trigger-gated doctrine

Ruled after a design conversation Graham opened by questioning A2 and proposing a dedicated tick service publishing to queues consumed by gateway AND quant. Two premises were corrected en route (A2 never flips the dependency arrow — caller-supplied bars is quant's own QR-D9 posture; and quant already has a Python STREAMING aggregator, so the duplication to avoid is specifically the tape-accurate HISTORICAL product), and one of Axium's own arguments was honestly weakened by the second correction. **The ruling: A2-WITH-SEAM** — a gateway `TickBarService` (module, not microservice) owns "Alpaca trades → policy-versioned 1s bars"; BOTH batch consumers (historic-sim scenario lane, quant 1s windows via caller-supplied bars) ride it; quant's `WindowSpec` drops `1s` (breaking, major bump). Accepted costs named in the register. **PX-D9 NEW:** Graham's fanout instinct adopted as trigger-gated doctrine, not built — graduate to a dedicated tape/bar service (the `TOPIC_BUS` broker-ready port's DA-D2 graduation) when a third live-stream consumer appears, the provider connection limit binds, or multi-user arrives. The seam is deliberately the organ that lifts out. Slices: PX-S5a (gateway seam) + PX-S5b (quant contract change) activated.

# [2026-08-02] arc+milestone | CAMPAIGN-02 (EP-40) BUILT — a campaign can WIN, its predictions are SCORED, and posture binds PER GROUP; six slices, ratchet never moved

The strategic tier completes its epistemics on the substrate CAMPAIGN-01 built. **C1** — a campaign can now *win*: `complete` joins the verdict union, and an optional `completion.machineTarget` auto-completes symmetric with falsifier auto-invalidate; success routes through the **same** probate + mandatory-rebirth path as failure (study V5), and a SIM fork that wins **completion-HALTs** exactly as one that dies (SOL-D3 symmetry). **C2** — the Tetlock move: dated, probability-bearing, deterministically-resolvable predictions ride the campaign wire into a per-agent **append-only ledger outside the artifact** (migration 034), resolve against daily closes at the authoring boundary (no new clock), and accrue a **Brier score computed on read**; `unresolvable` is excluded and visibly counted, never scored as wrong. **C3** — optional disjoint `scope.groups` carry a **subtractive** stance whose denial is linter-enforced at authoring; the deterministic gate is untouched and GS-D4 holds (scripts stay single-symbol). Slices #793–#798 → PRs #805/#804/#807/#811/#810/#814; migrations **034** + **035**.

**The ratchet never moved.** Across six slices — new schema, two migrations, new services, three modified hosts, a web surface — `plan-intent-gate.ts`, `decision-evidence-package.ts`, all **34 evaluator goldens** and `campaign-falsifiers.ts` are **byte-identical to `main`**, asserted from committed state at every slice tip and again after `origin/main` was merged into the arc mid-flight. A campaign using none of the new features behaves and serializes exactly as before.

**Honesty built structurally, not asserted.** `PredictionCalibration` is a discriminated union whose `available: false` arm **has no `brierScore` field** — an absence cannot be rendered as a number even by accident. The card's provenance is a three-way union (machine | judgment | **unrecorded**), read from migration 035's `terminal_cause` and never parsed from prose, where *unrecorded is not a third kind of decision but the honest statement that the record cannot say*. Exits are **structurally** never deniable: `DeniableActionKind = Extract<GameScriptAction,{kind:"enter"}>["kind"]` makes `"exit"` unnameable in the stance table, so no future stance can trap a position (CP-D7's probate depends on it).

**Four defects of one family, all caught in review, none of which would have failed a test.** (1) Rebirth stamped the predecessor's terminal status with a hard-coded `"invalidated"` literal — a **won** campaign would have been re-stamped dead on its way out. (2) The `{artifact, filings}` compile-time guarantee stopped one call short: `freshBirthForScenario` consumed the authoring *result object*, so a SIM fresh-birth's predictions vanished while the ledger looked correct. (3) Both lane price sources return the boundary day's **forming** bar, whose "close" is a last-trade-so-far — resolving against it would have made a verdict depend on *what time of day the boundary ran*. (4) The forward host's synthesized predecessor read model would have disagreed with the row the write beside it had just committed. **Generalizable:** a compile-time guarantee at a factory does not propagate through a result-object field, and any "latest bar" read on the current session is a forming bar, never a close.

**Register discipline.** CP2-D1..D5 ruled by Graham in the design conversation (D2 reinforced after an explicit ED-01 factoring pass → riders R1–R3: premise refs are one-directional, resolution rows store verdict-not-bars, conjecture and prediction surfaces are never blended). **B1–B5 ruled by Axium in-arc and stamped BEFORE the dependent lane was dispatched** — including **B5**, which rejected accept-prose for completion provenance because it contradicted a stamped constraint, and **B2**, the silent-drop fix. **B3** named a rung-1 coverage gap honestly rather than contorting a fence, and it **closed at S6's live smoke**. Follow-ups **FU-1** (a `holds` session cannot file), **FU-2** (`onResolveBy` on a non-trading date stays unresolvable — substitution rejected; the right fix is authoring-time refusal), **FU-3** (an unparseable stored predicate is silently skipped) carried forward, not built.

**Live proof + a named boundary.** One live birth emitted **C1 + C2 + C3 in a single authoring** — 3 filed predictions, a `completion.machineTarget`, and 3 disjoint stance-bearing groups: the arc's entire authored surface, live. Recorded honestly rather than overclaimed: the Completed read-back was driven through the **production** `setStatus` write, but its *trigger* was a harness replay rather than a live market condition — the deciding branch is unit-tested, not live-fired. Also observed live: two lab sessions **refused** rather than answering a verdict (`shape_invalid` from a prose preamble on the re-ask, and `lint_failed`) — real text-JSON-fallback behaviour, correctly billed as refused spend, and further evidence for the still-owed grammar-budget decision.

A2 keeps ONE aggregator and one exact-decimal/eligibility policy forever; A1 mints a second that must stay behaviorally identical. Awaiting Graham's call.

**PX-S2 BLOCKED — the collision Graham suspected is real.** `feat/campaign-02` modifies **both** PX-S2 target files: `campaign-initialization.service.ts` (+154; its diff includes the `@Inject(PolygonService)` line and a `composeLongitudinalEvidence` call site — the exact code PX-S2 rewrites) and `lab-game-script-session.service.ts`, plus ~1,000 lines of their specs, and it carries unreviewed WIP (S6, migration 035). Disposition: wait for CAMPAIGN-02, then repoint; re-check the collision at pickup. **[Re-checked 2026-08-03: CAMPAIGN-02 merged 2026-08-02, both call sites survive it, no in-flight branch touches either file — the block is DISCHARGED and PX-S2 is ready.]**

**PX-S6a LANDED (quant PR #29).** `_in_regular_session` moved from `data/polygon.py` to `data/market_calendar.py` (public `in_regular_session`); both Alpaca adapters repointed. Pure relocation, logic byte-unchanged, full suite green. This was the **hard blocker on ever deleting `polygon.py`** — both Alpaca adapters were importing a Polygon module purely for this predicate — and was required regardless of how PX-D3/PX-D4 rule, which is why it went first. Built in a worktree: the quant repo has **active parallel work** (`ed-01/s2a-discovery-lane`, uncommitted) whose tree was left untouched; ED-01 lives in `edge/`, this in `data/`, no collision.

# [2026-07-31] finding+cleanup | Cross-repo Polygon sweep — the platform and quant service ARE still calling Polygon; POLYGON-EXODUS-02 packet stood up

After ATLAS-03 made Atlas Polygon-zero, Graham asked for a final sweep of the remaining repos on the expectation that nothing still depended on Polygon. **That expectation did not hold.** Sweep (monorepo `655c2eb1`, quant `423bc82`), call-chain-verified:

**Monorepo — 4 LIVE paths**, all through `PolygonService` (the only egress; hand-rolled fetch, no SDK): (1) `/symbols/:s/overview|bars|brief|news` — the ASUMRT research panel, so its "Polygon" badge is truthful; (2) `/providers/status`, which does a real `getOverview("AAPL")` probe per uncached poll; (3) campaign initialization's longitudinal evidence (daily bars per universe symbol); (4) lab game-script `observeBars`. Paths 3/4 swallow errors into honest-degrade shapes, which is exactly why this drifted unnoticed — absence is silent, presence is live. **Quant — 2 LIVE paths:** 1-second REST bars (Alpaca's bars API has no 1s) and the `GET /reference/sectorMap` lane (`data/reference.py`, the only hard single-vendor dependency in either repo with **no port**). **Correction to a prior belief:** the 1-second *stream* is no longer Polygon — Alpaca IEX self-aggregation took it at APL-S4; only the REST/batch 1s path remains. Root cause is scope, not error: PS-D1 moved the realtime price origin and AHL-S4 the historic scenario bars — both price-lane migrations that succeeded; the survivors are a different family (reference/overview/news/1s/SIC) nobody had cause to look at.

**Fixed immediately (merged-pending):** `traidit` PR #808 — the **null-gated Polygon fallback** in `ProviderRecentScenarioService` (`?? new PolygonScenarioBarSource(...)` behind an `@Optional()` token: a fallback that reactivated by *removing* config, invisible in review; token now required and `PolygonService` left the constructor entirely) + deletion of the dead `probeHistoricalBars` cluster and the dormant `PolygonPriceSource`. Gates: lint 0/0, api typecheck, api suite 1295/1295. `traidit-quant-service` PR #28 — the env-isolation fixture stripped Alpaca creds to prevent real network calls from the suite but **not `POLYGON_API_KEY`**, so un-injected `create_app()` calls built a real `PolygonAdapter` + `ReferenceAdapter`; closed. Five dead `POLYGON_*` vars (zero readers) removed from the quant `.env`.

**Packet stood up: `docs/design/v2/price-substrate/polygon-exodus-02-packet/`** — survey + register **PX-D1..PX-D8 (ALL OPEN)** + conditional decomposition PX-S0..PX-S6. **The through-line:** two of the four genuine capability gaps (company overview, sectorMap) are things **Atlas already owns**, so finishing the exodus is substantially an *Atlas-adoption* task, not a vendor swap. The genuinely open question is quant 1s REST bars — aggregate (proven already in `alpaca-scenario-bar-source.ts`) or drop (a breaking wire change). PX-D6 proposes **keeping** the historical `polygon_massive` provenance vocabulary: provenance should record what actually happened, so "Polygon-zero" here means zero *calls*, not zero *mentions*. Definition of done is a verifiable property — the credential can be deleted everywhere and the subscription cancelled with no functional change, which is what finally discharges PS-D9.

# [2026-07-31] ruling | A3-D2 HARDENED — Polygon deleted from Atlas outright, no fallback provider

Graham, in-session: *"The new doctrine is polygon has to go. I don't even want it as a fallback. It's weight that I don't wanna have to deal with ever."* **A3-D2 is hardened** from "remove Polygon from Atlas" to **"no fallback provider exists."** This **supersedes the two-provider seam** that landed in `traidit-atlas` PR #12 (`feat/alpaca-price-lane`, merged 2026-07-30), which had deliberately retained Polygon behind `ATLAS_BARS_PROVIDER=polygon` — a decision made in parallel with, and unaware of, the A3-D2 ruling in PR #791. Docs-vs-code divergence surfaced rather than silently resolved (rule: docs are doctrine), then ruled by Graham.

**Deleted in `traidit-atlas` PR #14:** `polygon.py` · `PolygonClosesSource` + every provider branch in `bars.py` · `polygon_api_key` from `AtlasConfig` · `POLYGON_API_KEY` from `.env`/`.env.example` · the `data/bars_cache/` tree + gitignore entry · the Polygon fakes and comparison tests · every doc/comment reference. **The `bars.py` PORT is kept** (the seam is what made the vendor swap survivable and costs nothing; a future provider is a new class, not a re-plumbing) — but nothing sits behind it, and **naming the retired provider is now an error**, regression-guarded by `test_polygon_is_no_longer_a_provider` so an old `.env`/cron line stops the run loudly instead of deriving on a tape that no longer exists. Properties that had been asserted *as comparisons against Polygon* (non-common symbol-form coverage 542/542; the trading-days measurement basis) are preserved as standalone assertions rather than deleted with their counterpart.

Also in PR #14: a **live defect fix on `main`** found while rebasing — `derive_edges` picked its default window `end` off the UTC clock while `bars._eastern_end()` expands it to 23:59:59 ET, so between 20:00–23:59 ET the end instant is in the future and the free plan 403s the whole window (~4h/day outage, reproduced live). Fixed by choosing the default end in the Eastern calendar. Plus **A3-S3** (market-residualized correlation, new default — 60,905 edges, 56 market clones excluded) and **A3-S4** (N-PORT co-holding layer — 20,601 edges from a 2,204-ETF sweep, no price vendor involved). PR #13 closed as superseded (predated the bars port).

**PS-D9's Atlas gate is now satisfied outright** rather than "defaulted away from." Any future fallback provider is a new decision, not a dormant retained one. Scope fence unchanged: quant-service + monorepo Polygon lanes remain PS-D9's.

# [2026-07-27] merge+arc | QUANT-AUDIT-01 COMPLETE — the quant catalog 21 → 52 tools, the authoring pipe OPENED, and feed honesty became CONTRACT LAW end to end; register QA-D1..D9 all RULED

**The arc that started as "what can our quant service not see?" ends with a catalog more than doubled, six formerly-unreachable tools reaching agents for the first time, and a data-quality rule that no lane can quietly decide for itself.** Audit-first per Graham's 2026-07-26 activation: two read-only research lanes (repo reconciliation + a standard-practice Tier-1–4 scan) → three audit docs + a decision register → **no build until he ruled**. Record: `docs/design/v2/quant-audit-01/` (README · `quant_inventory_v0.md` · `blind_spot_audit_v0.md` · `augmentation_roadmap_v0.md` · `decision_register_v0.md` · two evidence appendices).

**The shape of the whole arc.** Packet PRs **#658** (spine + full synthesis) → **#670** (QA-D1..D7 stamped RULED, plus the GS-02 catch folded into QA-D1: *"engine VWAP is GS-02's build" is an ownership statement, not a gate exemption*) → **#691** (QA-D8 per-entry window identity, principle-now). Build: **Wave 1** = quant **#20** (the measurement substrate — ten scalar risk/return statistics on a pinned returns-prep spine) ∥ monorepo **#672** (**W-P P1+P2 — the authoring pipe**) · **Wave 2** = quant **#21** (nine regime-nature + cheap-structure tools) · the **pin slice** monorepo **#687** (contract 0.13.0, 40-tool catalog, dashboard twins, W-1/W-2 seed grants) · **Wave 3** = quant **#22** (four pair tools on the correlation-join + `ols_fit` spine) · **Wave 4** = quant **#23** (contract **0.15.0**, eight volume/liquidity/context tools — including the **first two `SOURCE_EVIDENCE` tools ever**, `outlierMove` + `barsAroundEvents`, per QA-D5 — plus the QA-D2 feed-declaration mechanism) · **QA-D2 enforcement** monorepo **#709** · and **this closing slice** (QA-D9 + the closeout). **Catalog: 21 → 52 registered tool ids.** Per-wave contract bumps throughout, additive-only, per the QA-D6 discipline — no new dependency was needed (the ADF significance stamp was deferred rather than dragging in statsmodels).

**The finding that reframed the arc: the catalog was never the binding constraint — the PIPE was.** Dashboards reached 21/21 tools; agent authoring resolved ≤15/21 and **seeded only two** (`rsi`, `relativeVolume`); the pair tool and all five cross-sectional tools were **structurally unreachable as agent evidence**. Several archetype PARTIALs (sector strength, peer sympathy) were pipe gaps wearing tool-gap clothes. QA-D7 ruled the pipe a Wave-1 *peer* rather than a follow-on, and #672 opened it — **six formerly-unreachable tools are now agent evidence**, and the operator's Industry-Lens/Market-Pulse context is no longer invisible to the mind authoring the plan.

**Feed honesty is now contract law, not per-lane judgment (QA-D2).** The free IEX feed is a single-venue slice carrying **zero auction volume**, and volume-derived signals are unreliable on it *precisely at trigger moments*. Rather than adjudicate that lane by lane — the status quo that had just bitten GS2-D14 — a feed-sensitive tool **declares** `feed:consolidated_tape_required` in its own `ToolContract.sourceDependencies`, and every lane withholds mechanically: the pull is not executed (the refusal precedes the network, exactly as a policy denial does), the frame metric is simply not supplied, `volumeFloor` resolves through its `onUncheckable` and fails closed — **with zero change to any file under `packages/agent/src/engine/`**, and the day SIP lands everything starts working with no code change at all. The enforcement is **data-driven from the registry, never a tool list kept here**; an unreadable registry is a stated third state, not a silent pass.

**QA-D9 — RULED (b), Graham 2026-07-27: an explicitly-declared SYNTHETIC feed QUALIFIES.** W-4 pinned the declaration on `relativeVolume` and `sectorAggregate`, which meant the SIM plan gym — a world with no feed to state — would have gone dark on every volume-derived reading. Graham's reasoning: the hazard QA-D2 guards is a **sampling** hazard, and **a fictional world is its own whole tape** — there is no larger consolidated tape it is a subset of, so the hazard cannot exist there; relative volume over a synthetic world is a true statement about that world, which is exactly what a simulated world is for. Three boundaries ride the ruling: **explicit declaration required** (nothing infers synthetic-ness from a scenario id or the absence of provider rows) · **unstated still fails closed** (and `synthetic-demo`/`fixture`/`mock` remain `unrecognized_feed`) · **nothing changes for real data** (`iex` still refuses, `sip` still qualifies, and a synthetic world qualifies as ITSELF — a separate recognized set, never folded into the consolidated-tape set, never recorded as `sip`). As built: the fixture loader is the **only** place in the repo that writes the literal, on a path whose bars come exclusively from committed fixture arrays, and both SIM read points resolve through one helper in which a provider load's own recorded recipe **wins** — so a fixture-side declaration can never describe provider bars.

**Registered tension, surfaced rather than averaged: QA-D9 × DA-D3.** The QA-D1 provenance label rides `assemblyNotes`, and `assemblyNotes` ride the planning book into the mind's payload — so a qualifying synthetic verdict labels the reading *"a declared synthetic world"*, which is a synthetic-vs-real **tell** in an agent-visible channel, the class of tell DA-D3 forbids on sim↔live parity grounds. The label stays true to QA-D1 (its being honest is the whole reason it is load-bearing); whether the note should split into a record-only channel and a blind agent-visible one is carried as an **open follow-up** on QA-D9 and flagged in `packages/agent/src/evidence/feed-qualification.ts`, not decided in code.

**Owed / carried forward:** the monorepo's generated-types pin is still **0.13.0** (40 tools) while quant `main` is **0.15.0** (52) — a pin-bump slice remains before the W-3/W-4 tools are typed here (the runtime registry read is live regardless, which is why the QA-D2 gate is already in force rather than latent) · the QA-D9 × DA-D3 channel question above · **#686** (promote the four feed-SAFE tools into GS-02's S1 socket) · QA-D4's frame-metric candidates remain a **proposal handed to GS-02**, whose lane they are. **Boundary held all arc:** zero files under `packages/agent/src/engine/` — the packet coordinated with GS-02 through the register, exactly as ruling 2 required.

# [2026-07-27] design+source | BYO MIND (EP-37 #693) — Alpha-phase direction captured: user-provided LLM keys/models as the agent mind, house key demoted to an opt-in bill-forwarded fallback

Founder conversation, same day the first `submit` flip armed the paper environment. The capture's headline: **no corner was painted** — the `MindProvider` port already routes every model call, the mind is already untrusted by construction (gate/guardrails/envelopes are provider-independent), the text-JSON + re-ask + honest-refusal path proven live this morning IS the universal provider contract (constrained decoding demotes to a per-adapter optimization), and EP-32's economics substrate already meters per `priced_model_id`. Realistic adapter map: Anthropic (exists) + ONE OpenAI-compatible adapter covers most of the market INCLUDING local (Ollama/LM Studio/vLLM speak it behind a base URL). Real work: secrets management (Kepler, conferred) · the fallback failure-taxonomy + consent + caps · **#682/#683 promoted to hard prerequisites** (refusal loops burning a CUSTOMER's tokens invisibly) · the minimum mind contract + mind-health surface · rides after Alpha auth/tenancy. Forks F1-F5 recorded not ruled (topology/local-reachability · fallback fee model + a COUNSEL resale-adjacency item for the legal register · per-user vs per-agent key scope · contract thresholds · adapter set). Record: `docs/design/v2/byo-mind/byo_mind_direction_v0.md`; board: EP-37 #693 (thin, milestone ALPHA). Nothing builds pre-Alpha.

# [2026-07-27] merge+milestone | GAME-SCRIPT-02 (EP-36) COMPLETE on `main` — PR #681 (`54fe8c3c`): **a volume-confirmed breakout is expressible, and a plan survives a market that moved overnight**; epic #649 + stories #650–#653/#655/#675 CLOSED

**The decision grammar grew for the first time since the exodus, and the evaluator's safety property survived it.** Post-merge census **by CONTENT** (the discipline squash merges force): `git diff --stat <gated-tip> origin/main` → **EMPTY** — main's tree is byte-identical to the tree the gate ran on. **34** plan-evaluator goldens, of which the **18 originals are byte-identical**; `plan-intent-gate.ts` and the DEP schema unmoved; **no migration** (GS2-D22 — `game-script@1` rides in `revision_json` byte-intact, so 024 was released to the #660 lane and consumed there). Six slices, register **GS2-D1..D22 all RULED**. Record: `docs/design/v2/game-script/game-script-02-design-packet/`.

**What agents can now express that they could not yesterday.** Rules read the **ATR, relative volume and pair correlation** the envelope could always read — the richest signals were wired exclusively to the pessimistic side, so a plan could say *"abandon ship if volatility leaves this band"* but not *"enter when volume confirms."* Levels can be **relative** (`authoringSpot`, `sessionOpen`, `openingRangeHigh|Low`) and are **derived inside the evaluator from consumed bars**, so they are replay-identical across SIM / lab / forward *by construction* rather than by discipline — the artifact-identity property V8 named as the under-claimed asset. The linter grew **symbolic reasoning** over those expressions (affine forms, three-valued, findings only on provable unreachability), so **authoring-time refusal survived the change** rather than degrading into refuse-to-run. And plans carry a **monotone PACE ladder** they descend and never climb — which conserves EP-35's re-plan budget, because descent is free and only exhaustion buys an LLM call.

**The ratchet INVERTED and held.** Every prior arc's safety property was "`packages/agent` diffs EMPTY"; this arc changes the evaluator deliberately, so it became **"the 18 original goldens byte-identical, no golden edited, asserted at every pushed tip"** — re-run independently by the integrator, never taken on a lane's report.

**Five findings, three about our own verification.** **(1)** The silent defect **two correct lanes created**: S2's reference discovery walked `script.rules`, empty by contract under a ladder, so a branch-bearing plan reported *no references at all* and would have run inert — no type error, no failing test, not even the honest-absent event built for that case. **(2)** The **compiled-grammar budget was already exceeded on `main` before this arc** — measured three ways by two lanes with different designs; plan authoring has been running on `anthropic-mind.ts`'s text-JSON fallback rather than constrained decoding. Degraded, not broken; **pre-existing, not arc-caused; the decision is owed.** **(3)** The honest-unknown guard **was a comment, not code** — a file promising *"never a silent drop"* had a bare `default: break` on its event path. Found only because the brief said to *verify* the promise. **(4)** A **ratchet assertion can itself be vacuous** — two lanes independently got a comforting EMPTY from a command that had resolved the wrong path list; both caught it by verifying the verifier, and the integrator's method was then sentinel-proved rather than assumed. **(5)** `packages/shared` has **no tsconfig**, so type errors in its specs are invisible to its own filtered test run.

**Named this session: the PLAY CALLER (P6).** Graham's ruling — the middle layer between LLM and evaluator takes the product name **Play Caller**, completing the tier metaphor **Campaign → Game Script → Play Caller**. It harmonises with vocabulary the design already used (each PACE branch is *"a full playbook"*), and the football analogue is exact: a play caller works from a **prepared script** and adapts live rather than reasoning from first principles. *"Sequencer"* is retained in the study as the **3T literature's** term so the Gat/Bonasso lineage stays findable. **Rename cost was zero** — caught before any code existed. **The caveat recorded with the name:** the layer is deterministic, cheap and model-free, and any document introducing it must say so, or the name invites the assumption that it is the "smart" tier when it is precisely the tier that makes most adaptation *not* require intelligence.

**Deliberately not shipped: `vwap`.** It *is* derivable (the same fold as `sessionOpen`) — the packet's original "needs plumbing" reason was wrong and is corrected — but it is volume-weighted, and IEX omits the opening and closing crosses, so an IEX-only session VWAP is **systematically** wrong rather than merely noisy. Per QUANT-AUDIT-01's QA-D1 as amended by this arc's catch, *"engine VWAP is GS-02's build" is an ownership statement, not a gate exemption*: every engine-side consumption vehicle sits behind the QA-D2 consolidated-tape gate.

**Three concurrent threads against one repo, zero collisions** (GS-02, EP-35 forward-testing, QUANT-AUDIT-01) — surfaces audited before dispatch, migration numbers claimed against `origin/main` and *released* when unused, and twice a lane **held rather than acted** on a claim it could not verify in an artifact. Both lanes adopted **"stamp before dispatch"** afterwards, from two instances of the same root: the record lagging the decision.

**Follow-ups:** **#686** (promote the four feed-SAFE quant tools — `adxDmi`, `percentB`, `rangeVolatility`, `rangeExpansion` — into the S1 socket; read it before minting a fifth condition kind, because `rangeVolatility` is an *input upgrade* to the shipped `volatilityWithin`, not a new condition) · **#648** (forward frames grants, FT's lane) · the `packages/shared` tsconfig gap · **the grammar-budget decision, owed to Graham.**

# [2026-07-27] build+arc | GAME-SCRIPT-02 (EP-36) **BUILT** on `feature/game-script-02` (integration PR is Graham's click): the expressive-plan arc — sensors, relative levels, the symbolic linter, and a monotone PACE ladder

**A volume-confirmed breakout is expressible for the first time, and a plan survives a market that moved overnight.** Six slices (S1–S6, PRs #656/#668/#669/#674/#677/#679) fleet-built by Marlow in isolated worktrees from doctrine-complete background briefs; Axium reviewed each line-by-line, independently re-verified every ratchet and claim, and merged each to the arc. Board: epic **#649**, stories **#650–#653, #655, #675**. Record: `docs/design/v2/game-script/game-script-02-design-packet/` (register **GS2-D1..D22**, all RULED). Source: `gs_campaign_evolution_study_v0.md` P1/P2/P4 under ruling V3.

**What exists now.** **S1 sensor symmetry** — `volatilityWithin` / `relVolumeAbove` / `correlationHolds` join the rule menu, closing the asymmetry where the richest signals were wired exclusively to the pessimistic side; **one predicate implementation, two callers** (envelope and rule share the comparison, proven identical by a test driving both against the same frame, not by inspection). **S2 derivable references** — `authoringSpot`, `sessionOpen`, `openingRangeHigh|Low`, derived *inside* the evaluator from consumed bars, so they are replay-identical across SIM / lab / forward **by construction**; the bare decimal string stays admissible (GS2-D6), so every stored artifact still parses. **S3 the symbolic linter** — `isConditionSetUnreachable` generalized from ℝ intervals to affine forms over a reference basis, three-valued `UNREACHABLE | SATISFIABLE | UNDECIDABLE`, **findings only on the first**: typed honest-unavailable applied to static analysis. **S4 PACE branches** — the monotone ladder, arming fresh on entry and safe *by construction* because a branch is never re-entered, `minDwellBars` hysteresis, a switch cage that breaks the envelope, and the **GS2-D12 exhaustion correction** (under branches the arming map holds only the active rung, so an exhausted Primary with unentered rungs below would have falsely raised `exhausted` and **bought a re-plan against EP-35's budget**). **S5 the read-back honesty floor** — the mirror stops lying by omission. **S6** — a watchpoint that cannot resolve its level says so, and names the branch.

**The ratchet INVERTED for this arc and held.** Every prior arc's safety property was "`packages/agent` diffs EMPTY"; this arc changes the evaluator deliberately, so the correct property is **the 18 original plan-evaluator goldens plus the 2 micropath goldens byte-identical, no golden edited, asserted at every pushed tip.** 18 → **34** goldens, all 16 additions. `plan-intent-gate.ts` and the DEP schema never moved: the evaluator describes, the gate disposes.

**Five findings, three of them about our own verification.** **(1) The silent one — the defect two correct lanes created.** S2's reference discovery walked `script.rules`, which under a ladder is **empty by contract**; a branch-bearing plan therefore reported *no references at all*, so every referenced level in a branch rule or watchpoint would resolve UNRESOLVED forever and its condition quietly never hold — **no type error, no failing test, and not even a `reference_unresolved` event**, because the announcement walker shared the blind spot. Found by S4's rebase; the flat path is unchanged by construction via `allGameScriptRules`. **(2) The grammar budget was ALREADY exceeded on `main`, before this arc** — measured independently by two lanes with different designs (S4 ran a pre-branch control; S2 ran a three-point series: main 9,775 B → REJECTED, arc base 10,783 → REJECTED, tip 14,268 → REJECTED). Plan authoring has been running through `anthropic-mind.ts`'s text-JSON fallback rather than constrained decoding; the fallback is real, specific and honest, so this **degrades rather than breaks** — but the register's dated `2026-07-18 / claude-sonnet-5` line is superseded and **the decision is owed to Graham.** GS2-D20's mandated re-probe worked exactly as intended: it caught a pre-existing regression, not a new one. **(3) The honest-unknown guard was a comment, not code** — `plan-readback.projection.ts` promised in its own header that unrecognized shapes render honestly, *"never a silent drop"*; conditions, sizing and assertions each had honest `'unknown …'` returns, and the **event** path had a bare `default: break`. The fallback that supposedly made event-side drift loud was itself silent. Found because the S5 brief said to verify the promise rather than rely on it. **(4) The ratchet assertion itself can be vacuous** — two lanes independently had a first run that silently resolved the wrong path list and reported a comforting EMPTY. Both caught it by **verifying the verifier** (append a known-differing file; confirm the command then prints). Axium's own method was tested against the same sentinel and is sound — but it had been *relied on* all arc without proof, which is the same error one level up. **(5) `packages/shared` has no tsconfig** and runs under `tsx`, so type errors in its specs are invisible to `pnpm --filter @traidit/shared test` and surface only when a consumer compiles it. A real type error slipped through exactly that way in S3 and was caught only by the full gate — the fast inner loop lies. Spun out as a follow-up.

**Sentinel probes, every slice, and the discipline paid.** The best were the ones that tested the tests: S1 de-shared the envelope predicate with a **boundary-only** difference and got **exactly one** red — the identity spec, with all goldens blind to it, proving that spec does real non-redundant work. S6 moved a field spread after `reason` — **same fields, different key order** — and got golden byte-mismatches, proving the goldens genuinely bind serialization order, which is what the whole inverted ratchet rests on. S4's dwell probe went red in one test and it **rejected that as insufficient**, realizing a count-only expectation passes under both readings, and rewrote it to assert the switch *bar*. S3's probe forced a real correctness fix: the emptiness rule needed `L ≥ U` with *either* bound strict, without which the forbidden axiom would have installed silently.

**Three packet corrections, all marked do-not-restore.** S1 **delivers the capability for EP-35 #632 follow-up item 2 but does not discharge it** — the forward call site is `forward-session-host.ts:470` on FT's branch, beyond S1's reach; tracked as **#648**, and closing it is *not* mechanical because frames project once before the re-plan loop while `script` is reassigned on re-author. **GS2-D1's vwap deferral stands but its recorded reason was wrong** — VWAP is derivable (the same fold as `sessionOpen`), so the real reason is **feed fidelity**: IEX omits the opening and closing crosses, making an IEX-only session VWAP *systematically* wrong rather than merely noisy, and a cheap-to-derive level that is quietly wrong is worse than one not shipped. **§1.3's UNREACHABLE claim was wrong** — `priceWithin(ORH, ORL)` is **UNDECIDABLE**, because the axiom is `ORL ≤ ORH` not `<` and a flat range leaves one satisfiable point; the strict axiom would make the linter **falsely refuse legitimate plans**, which is exactly what that slice's own sentinel probe exists to catch.

**Cross-arc coordination, three threads, zero collisions.** EP-35: audited before dispatch (their ratchet forbade the evaluator; ours forbade their surfaces), the one shared file uncontested, merge order agreed, migration 024 reserved and unused. QUANT-AUDIT-01: **a claimed ruling was held rather than acted on** because their merged register still read `OPEN` — the ruling was real and the artifact simply lagged it, and both lanes adopted **"stamp before dispatch"** as shared discipline afterward (their record-lagged-dispatch and Axium's `corpus-graph check`-without-`index` are the same root: the record lagging the decision). GS-02's reading of QA-D1 was **adopted into their ruling text**: *"engine VWAP is GS-02's build" is an ownership statement, not a gate exemption* — a level reference to VWAP is exactly as feed-sensitive as a `vwapDistance` condition, so every engine-side consumption vehicle sits behind the consolidated-tape gate.

**Owed to Graham at closeout:** the grammar-budget decision (pre-existing, not arc-caused, but now measured three ways). **Carried forward, gated on this arc:** P7 utility scoring with mandatory attribution, P6 the sequencer, and P3 feedback conditions — whose substrate is the plan's own consumed frame stream, **not** the campaign-tier daily-bar basket of #662 (confirmed to the FT thread; the two are different on every axis, so #662 is theirs alone).
