# [2026-07-26] design+source | GAME SCRIPT + CAMPAIGN EVOLUTION STUDY — the paradigm audited from the code, six structural gaps found, ten proposals; **PACE branches GREEN-LIT**, utility scoring RETAINED with mandatory attribution

**A design conversation, captured as elevated source material** (Graham + Axium, 2026-07-26, immediately after ALPACA-HISTORIC-LANE-01 landed). Graham's framing: the Campaign/Game-Script paradigm is the right move, but *"it probably needed to be looked at carefully and evaluated for its strength, to find where we can now leverage the paradigm we sit in."* Record: **`docs/design/v2/game-script/gs_campaign_evolution_study_v0.md`** — parse it when minting Game Script or Campaign capability work. It extends the 2026-07-22 capability trade study (now back-linked, and its **D.5 revised**).

**The audit, read out of the code rather than the docs — six structural gaps.** **(1) The sensor asymmetry:** the envelope can read ATR, relative volume and correlation; **rules cannot read any of them.** The richest signals in the system are wired exclusively to the pessimistic side, so a plan can say "abandon ship if volatility leaves this band" but cannot say "enter when volume confirms" — a volume-confirmed breakout, perhaps the most standard intraday setup there is, **is inexpressible today.** **(2) Levels are absolute literals** (`"191.25"`), computed from a spot already stale at session open; an 8 % gap makes every level nonsense and the only defence is envelope death — E1's authoring-spot seeding is a band-aid over exactly this wound. **(3) No feedback from the plan's own actions** — no condition reads unrealized P&L, bars since entry, or drawdown-from-peak, so **trailing stops and time-decay exits cannot be written at all.** **(4) A flat priority list conflates "which do I prefer" with "which world am I in."** **(5) There is no layer between the LLM (minutes, dollars) and the evaluator (ms, free)** — every adaptation must be compiled in advance or bought with a model call, though most need only arithmetic. **(6) A campaign can die but never win** — VERIFIED, not assumed: `completed` is in `CampaignStatusSchema` and migration 017, the verdict union is `holds|revise|invalidate`, and **nothing in the codebase ever sets it.** There is no positive signal anywhere in the loop.

**Cross-domain grounding** (the conversation's explicit brief was to look outside trading): the **options framework** (Sutton/Precup/Singh) — a rule cluster *is* ⟨initiation, policy, termination⟩, and the literature's results on interruption and intra-option learning apply directly; **3T robotics** (Gat/Bonasso) — we have the deliberator and the controller and are **missing the sequencer, which is where robotics concluded competence lives**; **scenario planning** (Shell) and **PACE** (military/wildland fire) — we have Primary and Emergency, missing Alternate and Contingency; **gain scheduling** — a controller tuned at one operating point degrades away from it; **utility AI** (Mark's IAUS); **Tetlock/Brier scoring** for the campaign thesis; and, as the warning label, **Allen & Karjalainen (1999)**, whose evolved rules failed to beat buy-and-hold after costs — *expressiveness without out-of-sample discipline manufactures overfitting.*

**Rulings from the conversation.** **P4 PACE branches GREEN-LIT.** **P4 and P7 are NOT alternatives** (Graham's "it's a dance" instinct, confirmed): branches answer *which world am I in*, scoring answers *which option is best in this world* — **layered arbitration**, exactly the 3T sequencer's two-part job, with a per-branch `selection: "ordered"|"scored"` policy defaulting to today's semantics. **P7 is retained, not deferred, and my first-pass caution is withdrawn:** given Graham's requirement to see how the numbers were arrived at, a scored system that records its full decomposition (axis · raw · normalized · weight · contribution) is **more** auditable than a priority list, which hides its reasoning in the authoring step. **Attribution recording is therefore mandatory, not optional** — and it makes *"what would have made those weights better"* computable by replaying episodes under candidate weights. **P5 + attributed P7 + EvalCal is a closed learning loop**, and shadow plans supply exactly the ground truth **DAE-D10** said empirical calibration was blocked on — at N× the rate of live trading, including for setups never taken.

**The branch/arming fork, resolved: a MONOTONE PACE ladder.** Arming enforces the `once` cage, and GS-D2 is explicit that an unintended re-arm is *"runaway repeat-buying — the classic conditional-order bug."* Reset-on-switch is thrashable; a global table leaves branches dead on arrival; per-branch freeze/restore is correct but multiplies the golden matrix. Making the ladder **strictly one-way** dissolves the fork: arming is **fresh on entry, safe by construction** because a branch is never re-entered. Falling past the last branch **is** an envelope break; climbing back up happens **only via a revision — and §7 already resets arming and resolves a broken script.** *"The storm passed"* is not a branch switch, it is a re-plan. Hardened with **`minDwellBars`** hysteresis (the standard anti-chatter device) and a **switch cage that breaks the envelope** — descending the whole ladder in one session is evidence the plan's model of the world was wrong, which converts a failure mode into a re-plan trigger and is **trade-study D.1 arriving through the back door.** Consequences recorded: `armAfter` may not cross branches, `ocoGroup` is branch-scoped, and a lower branch **inherits any position a higher one opened** (an authoring obligation, linter-warnable).

**Campaign.** **C1 — give campaigns a win condition**, with success routing through the same death/rebirth + estate-probate path as failure (Graham's ruling). **C2 — scored, dated, resolvable predictions** (Tetlock): the thesis becomes falsifiable in the sense already claimed, the agent accrues a real Brier score, and sizing can derive from **demonstrated** rather than claimed calibration. **C3 — Graham's pushback, and it reaches further than stated:** posture must bind **per symbol GROUP**, not campaign-wide, because one posture across a heterogeneous universe forces a square peg. A correlated-pair expectation is posture over a *declared relationship*, and **cross-symbol structure does not have to live in the script** — it belongs in the campaign, which is already the strategic multi-symbol tier. Single-symbol scripts inherit their group's constraint, **so most of the value of the GS-D4-deferred portfolio plans arrives without touching the one-script-one-symbol invariant.** Guardrails: posture stays **subtractive, never prescriptive** (keeps CP-D11 clean), and reuses the house `{ default, perSymbolOverrides }` shape.

**Sequencing:** **P1/P2/P4 are disjoint from EP-35 and parallelisable now** — FT touches migrations, `services/forward-testing/`, the Alpaca clock, `live-paper-host.ts`, campaign service calls, the worker and the gate, but **never `game-script-v1.ts` or the plan evaluator.** P5 and the campaign items wait for FT (host + campaign-service collisions). **Three complications recorded deliberately** so the doc is not only the parts that felt good: P2 makes the linter materially harder (authoring-time coherence checks must move to evaluation time or grow symbolic reasoning); branch × arming is a real fork, not a detail; and **P7 adds *fitted parameters* where P1/P2/P4 add *expressiveness*** — weight learning must be evaluated out-of-sample.

**On novelty, honestly.** Mechanisms exist elsewhere piecemeal; the **integration** is not known on the public market, and Graham's aggregate claim is accepted. The under-claimed asset is **artifact identity across environments**: the same `game-script@1` object runs on the same byte-identical evaluator in the Historic Simulator and in live paper, so a backtest result and a live result are statements about **the same object** — where most platforms carry a backtester and a live engine that drift, which *is* the classic "it worked in backtest" failure. Caveats kept: unpublished work is unobservable, and **novelty is worth nothing unless it produces edge — forward-testing's job to prove, not this document's.** Also recorded: the honesty doctrine (typed unavailable, no fabricated bars, honest-absent P&L with no colour field, sentinel probes) is **load-bearing rather than decorative** — every system that learns from its own record is only as good as that record's honesty, and three defects of exactly that class were caught in the preceding week.

**EvalCal validated by omission:** built a week earlier it would have been a calibration tool for per-decision confidence — the knob **DAE-D3 deleted permanently** as never-calibrated. Its actual job is now well-specified: the weight-and-attribution optimiser over recorded episodes, shadow counterfactuals and prediction Brier scores.

# [2026-07-26] merge+milestone | ALPACA-HISTORIC-LANE-01 COMPLETE on `main` — PR #628 (`e7c8203d`): **ALPACA IS NOW THE HISTORIC PRICE ORIGIN TOO**; the Polygon scenario rows are purged and the purge is durable; #565 CLOSED

**Both halves of the price line are Alpaca.** Phase 1 (#602, 07-25) took realtime; phase 2 takes historic. The Historic Simulator's scenario bars now come from Alpaca behind `ScenarioBarSourcePort` — **reversal is repointing one DI token**, and `PolygonScenarioBarSource` stays wired so that reversal is real. Merged by Graham as a squash.

**Post-merge census — by CONTENT, because a squash makes ancestry meaningless.** `git diff --stat <gated-tip> origin/main` → **EMPTY**: main's tree is byte-identical to the tree the gate ran on. Migration **021** present; all four new modules present; **zero executable** `provider === "polygon"` occurrences remain under `historic-simulator/`; Alpaca is the wired default (`app.module.ts:124`); `integrations-alpaca` is in **both** gates; **ratchet EMPTY** (the arc added nothing to `packages/agent`); main **boots**, DB healthy, `schema_versions` = 21.

**The deferred Polygon purge is DONE and DURABLE.** It was deliberately withheld before the merge — with Polygon still the wired default, the next scenario load would have re-created a row and silently undone it, producing a clean row count and no discharged obligation. On main: `POST /provider-data/purge {"provider":"polygon_massive"}` → **12 rows deleted**, receipt naming the true scope, **Alpaca's rows survived** (runbook §Verify step 3), a second purge reported **0** (idempotent). Then the proof the pre-merge run could not have given: a fresh scenario load on main produced a descriptor stamped `alpaca`, 390 Alpaca bars, and **no Polygon row was re-created**. The reasoning held in practice, not only on paper.

**PS-D9 gate 1 = PROVEN WITH RESIDUALS**, listed rather than smoothed: the 1-second tick path was never run end to end live; **auction-minute volume is unsolved and deliberately unguessed** (flagged `volumeSuspect`, which changes no value); S2's exclusion codes beyond `I` are unfalsified, not confirmed; no game-script run drove an Alpaca scenario into a DEP. **Gates 2/3/5/6 untouched — cancelling Polygon remains Graham's decision**, and the cross-provider evidence that would justify it is already banked, because that window shuts permanently at cancellation.

**THE CATCH OF THE ARC, and it was Vera's.** `@traidit/integrations-alpaca` had a `test` script and **98 tests that no gate had ever invoked** — `local-ci.sh` looped `shared agent api web`. She did not merely note it: she armed the naive-fold defect (`isAlpacaOhlcEligible` returning `true` — the exclusion set declared but never applied, the same class of bug that made policy v1.0 emit *no bar* outside regular hours) and **the entire merge gate stayed green**. Only the ungated suite failed. The hole predated the arc, but the arc had just moved the tick→OHLCV policy engine and the corporate-actions reach into that package — its own correctness core was sitting outside its own gate. Fixed in-arc: the package is now in the local loop **and** the CI matrix, sync-commented on both. **The generalization: when an arc moves code INTO a package, verify that package is actually gated.** She also verified the ratchet **per-commit, 24/24**, rather than at the endpoints, and re-armed both provider-equality traps herself to confirm the specs bind — which is precisely what an independent gate is for.

**Two more of her findings corrected before surfacing:** a doc-comment promising that each bar source clamps `limit` to its own provider ceiling — **neither does**, and the Polygon one deliberately cannot, since verbatim wrapping is what makes the reversibility proof byte-exact; and my own framing, which let "one line at two call sites" stand for a **10,921-line** review surface and omitted that **merging flips the default provider** — a real operator-visible effect. She was also right that "PROVEN" read as re-runnable when four of seven items were live measurements a reviewer cannot repeat; that distinction is now written into the evidence doc.

Record: `docs/design/v2/historic-simulator/alpaca-historic-lane-01/` — `arc_build_status_v0.md` (anchor + gate-1 evidence), `s4_live_verification_v0.md`, `cross_provider_audit_v0.md`, `s0_probe_findings_v0.md`. **#138 stays OPEN** for APL S2 (websocket) / S6 (trade-event topics), never built. **Next arc: #574** — the campaign↔execution seam, whose packet (#630) landed just before this and whose build was sequenced behind it.

# [2026-07-26] build+arc | ALPACA-HISTORIC-LANE-01 **BUILT** on `feature/alpaca-historic-lane-01` (integration PR is Graham's click): Alpaca takes the Historic Simulator's scenario bar lane, and an ingest recipe makes it reproducible

**Phase 2 of the Alpaca line.** Phase 1 (ASYNC-PRICING-LANE-01) made Alpaca the *realtime* price origin on 07-25; this arc makes it the *historic* one. Story #565, activated by Graham 2026-07-25 the moment the decision-architecture exodus landed. Six slices built (S0/S1/S2/S3 earlier, **S4 the keystone** PR #625, **S5** PR #627), S6 deliberately skipped, plus the runbook arm and closeout. Fleet-built: **Marlow** authored every slice in isolated worktrees from doctrine-complete background briefs; **Axium** reviewed each diff, corrected it, gated it, merged it, and ran the live verification. Anchor + gate evidence: `docs/design/v2/historic-simulator/alpaca-historic-lane-01/arc_build_status_v0.md`.

- **The arc replaces one line, at two call sites.** `ProviderRecentScenarioService` named Polygon directly; it now resolves `ScenarioBarSourcePort` through the `SCENARIO_BAR_SOURCE` token, with `PolygonScenarioBarSource` still wired so **reversal is repointing one token**. Downstream (`HistoricalBar` normalization, `barsBySymbol`, MicroPath, EST, the DEP) consumes the unchanged `ProviderHistoricalBarsResult`.
- **The honesty defect at the centre of it.** `sim-runner.service.ts` asked `bar.provider === "polygon" ? "observed" : "synthetic"` — so every Alpaca bar would have been stamped **SYNTHETIC**, a falsehood about real market data written into the valuation record and the DEP. Both traps are **exhaustive switches** now, not flipped ternaries: the negative-space form fixes Alpaca but silently promotes `unknown` to `observed`, whereas a switch makes the next provider a *compile error*. `unknown` → `fallback`, which the valuation engine resolves to a `degraded` posture — the honest can't-attest bucket.
- **Reproducibility is the load-bearing deliverable, and it is demonstrated, not asserted.** **Migration 021** records an ingest recipe on the row (schema, feed, window, interval, aggregation policy ref, corp-action basis, client version). Live against real Postgres and real Alpaca: hash the row, **purge it**, re-derive from the recipe — `bars_json` and recipe hashes come back **identical**, with `fetched: 1` proving a genuine re-pull. *"Store the query, not the data"* is now a property with a test.
- **Compliance rides the data.** Both vendors coexist in one table keyed apart — **no migration needed for coexistence** (`provider` is plain `text` leading the PK). A **provider-scoped purge** deleted the Alpaca row and left all 12 Polygon rows intact, receipt naming the true scope; a blank scope returns HTTP 400 and deletes nothing. The delete-on-termination runbook gained its **Alpaca arm**, which states Alpaca's duty as **prudential, not contractual** — Alpaca's T&C impose no explicit delete-on-termination obligation, and writing both arms as one duty would have misstated the posture.
- **The cross-provider audit — evidence that expires.** Run while both subscriptions were live, because it is unreconstructible after cancellation. Across **48 symbol-days**, **Polygon's minute bars fail to reconcile to Polygon's own daily bar by 14.80 % mean / 37.23 % worst, 48/48 in the same direction** (the missing volume is almost entirely the closing-auction cross — AAPL's 16:00 bar reads 75,171 at Polygon against 7,771,183 at Alpaca). Alpaca's equivalent gap: **0.42 %**. Polygon emits **fractional share volumes in 89.4 %** of minute bars; Alpaca in **0**. Consequence recorded as doctrine: **"matches Polygon" is not a valid volume oracle**, and an exact-integer volume assertion against Polygon bars can never pass.
- **The ratchet held on every slice** — `packages/agent`, the plan evaluator, the deterministic gate, the DEP schema and all goldens diff **EMPTY** vs `main`. Alpaca replaced a data origin; it never touched how decisions are made. local-ci **OVERALL: PASS** at each merge, re-run by Axium against the pushed tip rather than taken on report.

**Rulings made inside the arc.** **AHL-D2** (how prep is invoked) → operator-invoked prep with **store-as-checkpoint** resumability: each symbol-day is one row, so a crash leaves completed work persisted and a re-run resumes free — no job table, no run-state migration. **Migration 021 approved** only after verifying it was pre-declared by S2 (`tick-aggregator.ts:164`) and S3 (`corporate-action-adjustment.ts:100`) — arc plan, not scope creep — and built nullable with **no backfill**, so pre-021 rows read honest-`null` rather than carrying fabricated retroactive provenance. **S6 skipped** on the packet's own recommendation (defer behind DA-D4's measurable-delta question; #576 owns the dial).

**Gate 1 of PS-D9 is PROVEN WITH RESIDUALS, and the residuals are named:** the **1-second tick path was never run end to end live** (every live run used 1-minute grain); **auction-minute volume is still unsolved** (our opening-auction figure over-counts vs Alpaca's own bar; no single- or pair-code exclusion reproduces it, solved exhaustively — encoded as `volumeSuspect`, which changes no value, and deliberately not guessed at); S2's exclusion codes beyond `I` are **unfalsified, not confirmed**; and no game-script run drove an Alpaca scenario into a DEP. **The Polygon row purge was deliberately NOT run** — it is not durable while `main` still wires Polygon, so the next scenario load would silently undo it; it is the first post-merge step, with the command recorded and the mechanism already proven twice.

**THE DURABLE LESSONS.** **(1) A cited line number is one instance, never proof of uniqueness** — the packet cited one provider-literal pair, there were two; same shape as the two `getOrFetchScenarioBars` call sites the arc was built around. **(2) A port must not import its contract from the implementation it abstracts** — `ProviderHistoricalBar` lived inside `polygon.service.ts`, so the port and the Alpaca source both depended on the module PS-D9 exists to *delete*; repointed to a neutral home. **(3) `gh pr create` can succeed on top of a rejected push** — after a rebase, PR #625 briefly showed pre-correction commits; caught by comparing the remote ref against the verified local tip, and the habit is now *never trust that a PR shows what you tested*. **(4) Specs that hand-construct a controller are blind to unregistered DI collaborators** — two such specs passed happily with a missing provider while the spec that boots the real Nest injector failed outright. **(5) CLAUDE.md was stale in a way that offered a false escape hatch** — it claimed `ensureSchema()` runs inline idempotent DDL; it does not, and there are **zero** `add column if not exists` statements in `apps/api/src`, so migrations are the only schema path. Corrected. **(6) I doubted two of the builder's calls and was wrong both times** — verify before accusing.
# [2026-07-26] merge+arc | HONEST-SURFACES SWEEP COMPLETE on `main` — PR #624 (`f5bf2844`): what the exodus left pointing at nothing is retired; #616/#603/#607/#608/#609/#610 all CLOSED

**86 files, +686 / −2,224.** Four slices, each through its own reviewed PR into the arc branch (#618 H1 · #620 H4 · #622 H2 · #623 H3); Marlow built all four in isolated worktrees from doctrine-complete background briefs, Axium re-verified every zero-importer proof at slice tips and ran the final gate + live check. Two of the four "cosmetic" items were **live defects**:

- **The `'weaker'` verdict was unreachable — and it gates promotion.** `resolveEvidencePosture` conditioned both verdicts on a confidence delta; plan-derived DEPs carry `confidence: null` (DAE-D3), so the delta was always exactly 0 → `'stronger'` vacuously reachable, `'weaker'` permanently false. `resolveReadinessState` returns **`promotion_blocked`** for `'weaker'`, so **a candidate version materially worse on warning rate could not be blocked on comparison posture** — it degraded to `'mixed'`. Fixed by deleting the confidence term (H2), proven red→green with a test that failed first. `averageConfidence` is gone repo-wide; the DEP inspector's `'Confidence not reported'` chip KEPT (correct honest-absence for historical DEPs).
- **The `runner/`→`engine/` rename would have blinded the corpus graph.** Five docs carried `governs: ["packages/agent/src/runner/**"]`; post-rename every agent in `engine/` would get a false "no doctrine" from the rule-17 lookup. Fixed atomically in H4, split two ways: the four `status: doctrine` boundary-package docs repoint to `engine/**` (their concepts — gate/authority/decision layer — survived), but `runtime_architecture_v0.md` (a pre-exodus snapshot naming `decision-intent@1`/`trader-analyst@2`/the calibration gate) had its glob **removed** — aiming a stale architecture at live code is worse than blindness. Tooling gap filed as **#621** (`lookup <directory>` silently misses globs `lookup <dir>/<file>.ts` matches).

Also: H1 demolished the nine provably-dead workstation components (#603's ruled disposition; −1,318 lines; `primitive-preview` + its 8 primitives kept as deliberately-unmounted dev inventory with a header note; three-part zero-importer proof per target). # [2026-07-26] merge+gate | SPEC-TYPECHECK GATE HOLE CLOSED on `main` — PR #619 (`ce1310e6`): spec type coverage 61/245 → **245/245**; the 27 errors it was hiding repaired; #605 CLOSED, #617 OPENED

`apps/api/tsconfig.json` included only `src/**` + `packages/**`, so **`apps/api/test/**` was never typechecked** — a spec importing a deleted symbol passed `pnpm typecheck` and failed only at test run time (this is how the exodus's DX-3 fallout stayed invisible). Kepler wired `apps/api/tsconfig.spec.json` (`allowImportingTsExtensions` + `noEmit`, mirroring the web/domain-portfolio convention) into both `typecheck` scripts; `local-ci.sh`/`ci.yml.disabled` inherit with zero changes (they call bare `pnpm typecheck`). `noUnusedLocals` deliberately stays OFF — proven, not assumed: ESLint already errors on unused vars in specs (stdin probe), and enabling it would break the four intentional `_XNoDrift` compile-time drift assertions in `agent-economics-read.ts`. Closing the hole exposed **27 live type errors in 8 spec files** on `main` — mostly exodus fallout (signatures grew `agentId`/`campaignVerdict`; specs never re-typechecked *because the hole existed*). All repaired by Marlow with zero assertion softening (0 `as any`/`@ts-expect-error` added; the two fail-closed-guard fixtures route through the file's own `policyFixture` idiom — an escape hatch should be *named, localized, greppable*, never an anonymous inline cast). **The headline find is #617:** the live-paper order-client fake computed `filledAvgPrice: order.limitPrice ?? "106"` — wrong field name (`limit_price`), so it returned the constant for every limit order. Fixing it changed no test result; Marlow's **sentinel probe** (make the fake return a deliberately-wrong `"555"`) showed all 47 tests still pass → **no test in the LP-D8 limit lane asserts a fill price at all**, in the lane that submits real orders under Founder validation. Filed as #617, deliberately not fixed in a type-coherence slice.

# [2026-07-25] build+arc | DECISION-ARCHITECTURE EXODUS phase 2 — FLAT-LANE RETIREMENT **BUILT** on `feature/decision-architecture-exodus` (integration PR is Graham's click): the mind stops emitting decisions; it only authors plans

**Game Script is now the sole decision vocabulary.** The pre-Game-Script lane — the mind emitting one flat `decision-intent@1` per opportunity, per cycle — is retired end to end, so **every DEP is plan-derived**. Story #450 under epic #525, activated by Graham 2026-07-25; phase 1 (host unification, PR #522) landed 07-21. Design conversation → packet (register **DAE-D1..D10 RULED**) → 6 build slices + closeout. Fleet-built: **Marlow** authored all six slices in isolated worktrees from doctrine-complete background briefs; **Axium** reviewed every diff line-by-line, merged each into the arc, and ran the live verification. **Net: 153 files, +2,373 / −10,339 — a 7,966-line subtraction.**

- **DX-1** (#585, −338): the flat lane's web surface had **zero production callers** — the SIM "Run agent" button was already re-pointed to the authored game-script endpoint. Also removed the **"Decisions" cadence dial** per **DAE-D6**: it rendered three Evaluation Policy options whose selection was silently discarded (issue #583). Removed, not relabelled — on the surviving lane *the plan evaluator IS the cadence*, so there is no operator choice to present.
- **DX-2** (#586, −1,663): `POST /runs/trigger` + `POST …/agent-run` retired; **`RunsService` becomes a pure read service** (7 DI deps → 3).
- **DX-3** (#587, −2,576): the delicate one. `sim-environment-adapter.ts` (863) + `lab-environment-adapter.ts` (535) are *named* for the flat lane but **export Game-Script-critical symbols** — so **DAE-D7** ruled extraction-before-deletion. Four symbols moved to neutral homes with every importer re-verified, then the flat halves died. A **fabricated artifact** went with them: the lab evidence provider's caller was passing `priceBook: new Map()` purely to satisfy a type.
- **DX-4** (#588, −4,117): the flat runner, the calibration gate, the flat mock mind, the whole `trader-analyst` family, and the `decision-intent@1` **emission contract**. **DAE-D1** is the precision that made this safe: `decision-intent@1` named *two* things — the emission contract (deleted) and the intent **vocabulary** that plan-derived intents are made of (**kept**; `plan-intent-gate.ts` and the DEP schema are built on it). Reading epic #525's one-line thesis literally would have broken the surviving lane.
- **DX-6** (#590): **migration 020** purges the legacy residue per **DAE-D2** (Graham: *"useless weight — purge it"*), plus four columns dead since #101; `agent_lane` enums/unions narrowed. Departs from the SIM-MOD §0.5 dark-by-design precedent **deliberately and with reasons recorded**: §0.5 preserved *real* accumulated history; here there were ~40 scratch rows.
- **DX-5** (#589): the **confidence floor deleted permanently** (**DAE-D3**, Graham's option (iii)) — `confidenceThreshold`, `abstentionBias`, calipers **DC-CALIB-001 and DC-CALIB-002** (registry 115→113), the Agent Creation Step-5 knob. **The reason outlives the decision: it was never *calibrated*.** "Calibration" names a feedback loop between reported confidence and realized outcomes; none ever existed. It was a hand-set floor on a scalar the model self-reported about itself. Conviction is now **structural** — which rules an agent authors at all, each rule's sizing, the campaign's machine falsifiers, the judgment falsifier.

**The ratchet (DAE-D4 — this arc is a pure subtraction), asserted on every slice and re-run at the tip:** the plan-evaluator core + deterministic gate + DEP schema diff vs `main` is **EMPTY**; **no Game Script golden was edited** (all 17 are game-script; zero flat-lane goldens ever existed); boundary flags unchanged; **#562's live-paper execution lane is byte-identical to `main`** — this arc never touched the code that places real orders. local-ci **OVERALL: PASS** at the tip (shared 431 · agent 285 · api 635 · web 1478).

**Live verification against the real DB**, on the three slices where unit fakes are weakest: an authored game-script scenario run **end-to-end through the freshly split adapters** (`ok: true`, 7 plan-derived DEPs, `session_id` stamped, **zero** `source_kind='decision'` rows — DAE-D8 confirmed in practice); a full Agent Creation walk 1→7 to **checkpoint v0.2**; and the purge proved by **row identity** (surviving run-ids + DEP decision-ids snapshotted before and re-listed after, byte-identical), with two boots plus a forced SQL re-application proving idempotence.

**THE DURABLE LESSON — four of the packet's own claims were FALSE, and the register now records them.** A **corrections log** in `decision_register_v0.md` captures all four, incl. **DAE-D5's premise**, which was wrong in a *ruled* item: it asserted `promptTemplateId` was flat-only, but `game-script-v1.ts:355` carries `authoredBy: MindBindingSchema` as **plan provenance** and both surviving lanes stamp it — executing the ruling literally would have **stripped authorship lineage**. Amended in place *with the original struck through, not rewritten* (a register records what was ruled AND what was wrong with it); the fix splits `MindBindingSchema` (manifest posture) from `AuthoringMindBindingSchema` (authorship lineage), **byte-identical on the wire**. Three failure modes generalize: **grepping field ACCESS misses schema COMPOSITION**; **"flat-lane by producer" is not "flat-lane by assertion"** (a test whose producer is dying may assert the survivor's invariants); and **shared vocabularies wear the retiring lane's name** (`'agent'`, `micropath`, `runner/` each named two things — one dying, one surviving). **In a deletion arc the inventory is a hypothesis; the importer graph is the truth.** Every one was caught by the **grep-proof-before-deletion** requirement in the slice briefs, and independently re-verified by Axium before merge — never by the census itself.

**What the system gained:** one decision vocabulary; **the Campaign tier becomes universal** (the flat lane was campaign-blind — no authoring boundary, so a flat-lane agent structurally could not carry a standing thesis); and **interpolated decisioning is dead** — it existed only because a mind needed to be asked something between observed bars, so the Alpaca historic tick→bar lane (**DAE-D9**) now removes *a producer whose consumer is already gone*. Two gate/UI defects folded in (#583 dial, #584 dead contract id). Follow-ups minted: **#605** (spec files are never typechecked — a real gate hole), **#607** (orphaned Agent Details workbench panel), **#608** (permanently-empty confidence surfaces), **#609** (archetype seeds naming retired concepts), **#610** (dead evaluation-policy symbols + the `runner/` directory name).

**Boundary:** no execution-authority change. Order-submission doctrine (#530) untouched and unreferenced. Record: `docs/design/v2/decision-architecture-exodus/flat-lane-retirement/` (README · register + corrections log · decomposition · arc_build_status).

**Phase 1 of the Alpaca-replaces-Polygon line is real.** Graham merged the integration PR #602 (monorepo) and quant PR #19 (sibling) within two minutes of each other — the platform's realtime price origin is now **Alpaca free IEX ($0)**, end to end: the ticker/tiles, the LIVE decision lane, and the quant realtime compute stream. Realizes the price-substrate rulings **PS-D1 = A · PS-D2 = A · PS-D6 = A** (2026-07-24). Designed → decomposed → built → merged inside one day, fleet-orchestrated: **Marlow** authored all four monorepo slices in parallel isolated worktrees, **Tycho** the quant observer, **Vera** ran the independent gate, Axium reviewed every diff line-by-line and merged the slice PRs into the arc branch under standing feature-branch authority (Graham clicked arc→main).

- **APL-S5 env hardening (#581, PR #594):** `load-env.ts` now applies dotenv-standard inline-comment semantics — an unquoted ` # comment` is stripped (the `ALPACA_BASE_URL=… # Change to live later` P0 hazard that silently corrupted the URL is dead), a `#` inside quotes or without preceding whitespace stays content, and the EP-06 present-but-empty override semantics are preserved untouched. New `boot-env-check.ts` logs the resolved feed + data base URL at boot (values only — keys never read) and **warns legibly when `sip`/`delayed_sip` is configured** (fail-honest at boot, not a mystery runtime 403).
- **APL-S0 market-data client (#577, PR #595):** `AlpacaMarketDataClient` in `packages/integrations-alpaca` — `getLatestPrice` + `getRecentBars` against the **data** host (never the ordering host), feed-parameterized (`iex` default; `sip` changes only the query param — SIP-readiness without SIP). **Byte-exact decimal preservation** for the headline price (guarded wire-literal extraction, falling back to the value-faithful shortest round-trip) so a trailing `125.0600` survives; typed honest-unavailable for keyless/403-entitlement/429/no-data/network/malformed; 20 fixture tests incl. a secrets-never-leak suite. Zero order/account coupling (DA-D1).
- **APL-S1 `AlpacaPriceSource` + the swap (#578, PR #598) — the $0 real-time milestone:** the port impl (`source = "alpaca"`) plus the **one-line `PRICE_SOURCE` repoint**; `PriceIngestService` and every consumer untouched, `PolygonPriceSource` left wired so reverting is repointing one token. **Ticker honesty recast:** the strip's stale `delayed` affordance (~15-min, market-data tier) became a quiet `iex` source note titled *"Real-time IEX quotes (single-venue feed)"* — show-don't-tell, no banner; the `delayed-note` class renamed `source-note` because a class named "delayed" for real-time content is a stale-semantics lie.
- **APL-S3 decision `frameSource` → Alpaca (#579, PR #600) — LP-D3 CLOSED:** the live-paper loop's decision frames now come from Alpaca 1Min IEX bars, so **every DEP stamps `priceEvent.sourceProvider: "alpaca"`** — decision and order finally speak one provider (before this, decisions priced on Polygon ~15-min-delayed bars while orders filled at Alpaca-live prices). **Orientation finding that reshaped the slice:** the LP spike's `runLivePaperHost` had **no production caller** — frames were supplied by tests — so S3 *added* the production frame-sourcing entry (`runLivePaperHostFromAlpaca`) rather than re-pointing an existing projection. The provider-neutral adapter, `runGameScriptHost`, and the entire order path are diff-verified UNTOUCHED; the lab/SIM Polygon paths stay put (later PS-D9 gates).
- **APL-S4 Alpaca `BarObserver` (#580, quant PR #19, `ffa37207`):** a third impl of the `BarObserver` Protocol serving the **1s realtime compute lane** by self-aggregating per-second OHLCV from Alpaca's IEX trade stream; endpoint selection **prefers Alpaca when keyed and falls through to the Polygon delayed cluster unchanged when not** (keyless labs behave exactly as before). **Contract-additive:** `/stream/compute` frames byte-compatible, `CONTRACT_VERSION` unmoved, the monorepo consumer unchanged; `/ready` gains a `realtimeBars1s` lane value inside the existing index signature. Deliberate honesty calls: **no cross-provider REST seed** (splicing consolidated-SIP seeds onto single-venue IEX bars would silently change the series' measurement basis mid-window — the cost is an honest warm-up instead of confidently-wrong numbers); **closed-seconds only** (the forming second is never served); the free-IEX **30-symbol cap** degrades per-symbol with a typed `ProviderSymbolCapExceeded` reason (a PS-D2 trigger surface), never a silent truncation.

**Verification.** local-ci at the arc tip **OVERALL: PASS** (shared 454 · agent 359 · api 677 · web 1489 · lint · typecheck · migrations · web build) and **re-run green on `origin/main` after the merge** (rule 18). **Vera independent gate: PASS 9/9** — including both ratchet checks EMPTY (`packages/agent`, `packages/shared`, and the game-script adapters untouched: the evaluator/DEP-schema/gate ratchet held through a provider swap) and a no-fabrication sweep of every production path. **Live browser verification (Axium):** the workstation running arc code showed the boot line `Alpaca market data — feed="iex", base="https://data.alpaca.markets"`, all 7 universe symbols priced with the `iex` affordance, zero console errors, and the wire proof — every SSE envelope stamping `"source":"alpaca"`. **Post-merge census:** merge SHAs confirmed ancestors of their mains, all slice artifacts present on `main`, and the arc verified intact against the **V1 Exodus (#601)** that landed immediately before it (V1X touched two web files, neither on this arc's surface). **Honest caveat recorded:** merge day was a Saturday — prices were truthfully static at Friday's last IEX prints, so the *ticking* demo and the S3 forward-test headline proof (a paper run's DEP + fill on the same provider) are owed at the next market open; Graham clicked on the code-path + wire + unit evidence with that stated.

**Boundary.** A **pricing** lane only — no order-execution scope change (submission stays under #530: founder unrestricted, non-founders gated). **$0 throughout**: `iex` everywhere, no slice sets `sip`; the SIP ($99) flip remains Graham's Founder-Validation-onset trigger (PS-D2), a config event, not code. Reversible by one DI line.

- **A2-S4 — atomic edge re-derivation (`traidit-atlas` PR #8):** migration 008 adds `run_id` lineage (FK→`atlas_refresh_run`) on `atlas_listing_edge`. New `edge_refresh.py` frame — the old unconditional `_upsert_edges` (DELETE-then-INSERT, no pre-delete validation) is REMOVED; `derive_edges.main()` now **validates the candidate edge set before touching gold** (non-empty floor + 50% shrink-cap vs last-good + provenance) then DELETE+INSERT+record-run in one txn; rejected gate → DELETE never runs; mid-promote failure → rollback restores last-good (works because `_open_run` self-commits the run row). Network-free `edge_rederivation_check` (never_derived>constituents_changed>cadence>up_to_date; new/delist/symbol_reuse/cik_merge trigger, ticker_change correctly does not) via `atlas-derive-edges --check-only|--if-needed`. Bars stay ephemeral (ATLAS-D2/D13). 49 tests incl. mid-promote-rolls-back-to-last-good.
- **A2-S5 — cadence scheduler + `/refresh/status` seam (`traidit-atlas` PR #9):** `atlas-refresh-scheduled` entrypoint driven by a **dumb daily cron heartbeat** — cron timing deliberately non-load-bearing; the tested Python core (`cadence_due`/`what_is_due`, tiers weekly 7d/monthly 30d/quarterly 90d, on-demand `classify` never clock-fired) decides what fires; a missed day self-heals. Failure surfacing via `source_status()` → per-source verdict (current/stale/failing/never, never silent) exposed at **`GET /refresh/status`** on the Atlas serve API — the cross-repo seam S6 consumes (ADR-009). No migration (reads S1 tables). `infra/scheduler-runbook.md` + `infra/atlas-refresh-scheduled.sh`. 68 tests.
- **A2-S6 — staleness surfacing / honesty (monorepo PR #509):** extends the honest-degradation Atlas seam with `getRefreshStatus()` + `GET atlas/refresh-status` (separate cached read, same TtlCache/`available:false` envelope — Atlas down never fabricates freshness); the ASUMRT window footer shows a single quiet **"Reference data as of ⟨date⟩"** line (pure `reference-freshness.model.ts` projection; show-don't-tell — no chip+banner). Four verdicts + unreachable, each carrying a price-boundary note (reference-only, never implies price freshness — ATLAS-D2). Date anchor = **oldest promoted `as_of`** across sources (honest floor). No migration.

**Verification (Axium rule-18, per slice + arc):** traidit-atlas has NO CI, so gates were Axium's — `uv run ruff check` / `uv run pytest` / `uv run atlas-migrate` (idempotent) independently re-run on every slice; live schema assertions (partial index present + blanket constraint dropped; run_id column+FK; migration idempotency). **S6 was LIVE-BROWSER-verified end-to-end:** Axium stood up the full cross-repo stack (Atlas API :8000 on main + monorepo API :3000 wired + worktree web :4200) and drove the real ASUMRT window through all three reachable states — `never` (empty DB) → "Reference data has not been refreshed yet"; seeded promoted runs (one 5d-older) → `current` → "Reference data as of ⟨older date⟩" (proving the oldest-`as_of` FLOOR live); killed Atlas + cache-cleared → "Reference data freshness unavailable" (honest reason); zero console errors, no horizontal overflow, seeded rows deleted after (dev DB left honest). Monorepo gates: typecheck (8 projects) + lint clean, api suite 502/502 (independent). Vera folded into Axium's own live gate for this single-footer slice (verified more thoroughly live than a spec-runner).

**Two observations surfaced to Graham (neither a blocker; both flippable/optional):** (1) **Date-anchor ruling (Axium, decision-authority):** kept the OLDEST promoted `as_of` as the headline anchor — the honest floor that never overstates freshness; tradeoff is that a quarterly source (ETF census/holdings, up to ~90d) can drag the headline date old even when the core weekly listings are fresh. One-line flip (`oldestAsOf()`) to anchor on the identity layer if Graham prefers the headline track the weekly universe. (2) **Latent S5 edge:** `overall_verdict` is worst-of-ALL sources including on-demand `classify`; a `classify`=never (cold-start) can drag overall to `never` even when everything scheduled is fresh — correct/honest for the current dev DB (nothing has a tracked run) and a non-issue in prod (classify runs at build), so a cold-start artifact; optional small S5 follow-up = exclude on-demand-never from `overall_verdict`.

**Boundary (unchanged, ATLAS-D2/D3/D8):** reference data only — NO prices (Polygon stays the vendor price layer, live/separate); public-domain/CC0 sources only (SEC EDGAR, Wikidata CC0), provenance (source + as_of) on every row; phase-bound legal posture unchanged (solo, non-commercial, paper/sim), the written review + commercial re-clearance still owed but not a blocker. Refresh introduces no new source classes.

**Keys note:** the reference sources need ZERO API keys (only a User-Agent contact string; hardcoded default). The ONLY credential in the whole system is `POLYGON_API_KEY`, used exclusively by `derive_edges`/A5 (the price-bar-derived correlation neighborhoods — the vendor layer). Graham is adding it to the `traidit-atlas` `.env` (already in the quant-service + monorepo .envs; `.env.example` scaffolds it). **Deferred LIVE eyeballs still owed by Graham** once the key lands (none block the arc): `atlas-refresh --all` near-no-op, `atlas-derive-edges`, and a scheduled run — all need outbound network + Polygon, unavailable in the build/verify sandboxes.

**Board:** epic **#501** (TRAIDIT-ATLAS-02) + all six stories **#502–#507 CLOSED/Done**. **Records:** the Atlas-02 design packet `docs/design/v2/traidit-atlas/traidit-atlas-02-refresh-packet/` (README, `atlas02_refresh_direction_v0.md`, `decision_register_v0.md` A2-D1..D10 RULED, `refresh_implementation_decomposition_v0.md` A2-S1..S6); the `traidit-atlas` repo carries the pipeline + migrations 006–008 + `infra/scheduler-runbook.md`. **Open Atlas follow-ups (beyond this arc):** Nasdaq/all-US expansion = **Phase-3** (separate); the written legal review (ATLAS-D8 re-clearance gate for commercial/LLC); data-quality deferrals (~427 missing ETFs, CUSIP/ISIN crosswalk, exchange-label normalization); the two optional refinements above. **Next priorities:** Graham's board queue — EP-32 (Agentic Unit Economics) is the recommended-next docket item (engineering-design packet stood up 2026-07-19, not yet activated); #143 Algo real-time consumer (async/realtime layer) also in flight-adjacent.

# [2026-07-20] correction | Data-rights: per-second granularity is NOT a licensing gate — red herring retired (Graham founder call)

**A "per-second data re-triggers data-licensing / professional review" flag had propagated** — from a quant-thread inference into the QS-06 closeout docs (PR #497, merged), a follow-up task chip, and an Axium cadence rationale. **On Graham's challenge, it was checked against the two governing docs and found ungrounded:** neither `docs/context/governance/data-rights/massive_compliance_v0.md` nor the professional-questions register (`§Trigger bindings`) gates on aggregate granularity. The recorded founder determination gates on **real-time (non-delayed)/options** (exchange agreements), **multi-user/commercial** (Business tier), and **durable persistence** (Phase-1 controls) — never bar size. Our Starter Individuals tier entitles 1-second *delayed* aggregates (probe-confirmed), so consuming/displaying them single-user + ephemeral is the **same posture as 1-minute**. Corrected (2026-07-20, PR to follow): `massive_compliance_v0.md` (source clarification), `CURRENT_STATE.md`, `algo_quant_service.md`, and the QS-06 log entry below. **Not a legal conclusion** — this aligns the flag with the already-recorded founder determination; the genuinely-open gates (real-time entitlement, multi-user, persistence) stay open. Consequence: the 1m-vs-1s cadence choice is now a pure compute/volume + UX call, not licensing-constrained.

# [2026-07-18] tooling | File-tree snapshot scripted + truncation-guarded — `scripts/snapshot-file-tree.sh` replaces the paraphrase-prone raw `tree` command

**Root-cause fix after a Rin closeout run (PR #464) truncated the file-tree snapshot.** The session-closeout snapshot (workflow step 8) was documented as a raw `tree` one-liner in prose; on a real run the librarian paraphrased it and a `-L 3` depth limit crept in, silently shrinking `current_file_tree.txt` from ~3,500 lines to ~100 and dropping the whole `docs/` corpus. Rather than hand-hold per dispatch, the command is now a single named script `scripts/snapshot-file-tree.sh` (mirroring how the repo already wraps its migration-collision and corpus-graph guards): no flags to get wrong, resolves the repo root itself so it runs from anywhere, and a **floor guard** that FAILS LOUDLY and refuses to overwrite if the output is under ~1,000 lines (which also catches a missing `tree` binary) — a truncated run can no longer masquerade as a legitimate large diff. Doctrine repointed at the script: `context_maintenance_workflow.md` step 8, `context_librarian_checklist.md` Session Closeout, and the Rin harness process line (`updated` dates bumped; corpus content sidecar regenerated, `check` + `index --check` green). Guard verified to fire (fake truncating `tree` → exit 1, original snapshot preserved). Tooling/doctrine only; no application source touched.

# [2026-07-18] merge+board-sync | GAME-SCRIPT-01 ARC COMPLETE on `main` — PR #444 (`31353c4f`): all 10 slices + 2 fixes, Vera PASS + live re-smoke PASS ($0.0148), Game Script now the decision architecture; #442 + EP-32 structure

**Graham merged the GAME-SCRIPT-01 arc** (PR #444 → `main` `31353c4f`, 2026-07-18). **Game Script IS the decision architecture now:** agents author declarative `game-script@1` plans; the runner's deterministic plan evaluator executes them against incoming bars at machine speed, emitting derived intents through the unchanged budget/risk gate. All 10 slices (GS-1..GS-10) merged + 2 arc-blocking fixes (#440: Anthropic streaming always-on, clears the SDK's 10-min non-streaming guard; #441: provider-recent scenario alignment via `alignRangeToObservedBars`). **Verification:** integration gate on the merged tree (typecheck + build clean) + **Vera independent PASS** + **live re-smoke PASS on real Polygon data** (1-min + 1-sec bars, two lanes, full plan authoring → evaluation → fill → DEP lineage carrying `planRef`), actual cost **$0.0148**. **Honest finding (NOT a defect):** live authoring on single-session scenarios yields watchful-waiting (no firing rules) because the authoring window is one bar. No lookback → indicators uncomputable → the mind honestly declines conviction. This is truthful behavior, not a bug. # [2026-07-17] merge+board-sync | WALLET ARC (EP-30 #364) COMPLETE on `main` — integration PRs #377 + #380 merged (`7029be07`): all three capital environments real (Sim functional + Paper functional + Live read-only + ALLOCATE stub); two UI doctrines codified; direction §5 reconciled; migrations 008/009; stories #365/#368 closed, W3/W4 inline to arc PRs (no separate stories created)

**Graham merged the Wallet arc in two PRs** (verified via git this pass; `main` at `7029be07`, **integration PR #380** the final merge, 2026-07-17): the **Wallet — capital allocated to agents (EP-30 #364) is CODE-COMPLETE on `main`**, shipping W1–W4 (four slices), two UI doctrines, and the full two-layer capital model across Simulator/Paper/Live environments. The arc is the first operational application of the new **UI-copy discipline doctrine** (`ui_copy_discipline_show_dont_tell_v0.md`) and the **label capitalization canon** (`label_capitalization_and_casing_canon_v0.md`).

**Delivered (task→integration Axium, integration→`main` Graham per rule 15; live-verified each slice end-to-end through full reload + Postgres round-trip, zero console errors):**

- **W1 (PR #377)** — **Wallet window + glyph + Live read-only:** a dedicated wallet glyph in the top-right toolbar (beside notifications / gear / user-icon) opens a modeless non-detachable Wallet window (mirrors Settings modality). Three tabs: Simulator / Paper / Live. Live tab renders the brokerage `AccountSnapshot` balance read-only (from Alpaca if creds present, else mock). Migration **008 `wallet_config`** table establishes the persistence spine (keyed by environment, exact-decimal `numeric(20,2)` money, string end-to-end).
- **W2 (PR #377)** — **Simulator sub-view functional:** operator-editable standing defaults that every new Simulator run draws from. Seeded to doctrine: 1000 USD · 100% allocation · 0% reserve · equal-weight cap. `GET/PUT /wallet/simulator` — persists to `wallet_config`, full round-trip verified (set → Save → reload → persisted; bad bounds → 400).
- **W3 (PR #380)** — **Paper sub-view functional:** per-agent hard-cap budgets (the two-layer allocation model's ALLOCATION layer). Migration **009 `wallet_agent_budget`** (per-agent, FK `lab_agents` ON DELETE CASCADE, exact-decimal, CHECK admits paper + live). `GET/PUT /wallet/paper`: agents LEFT JOIN budgets (unsaved seed to default, nothing written until Save); bulk atomic upsert; agent ids pre-validated → friendly 400; money is string. Web: store Paper draft + load/patch/save; agent-list UI with per-agent money input + one **Save budgets**. Default seed **$100,000** per agent (standard paper account v0 default, revisitable).
- **W4 (PR #380)** — **Live sub-view ALLOCATE stub (frontend-only, no backend):** completes the brokerage-proxy three-verb boundary (READ · ALLOCATE · MOVE). READ ✅ (from W1, real if Alpaca creds). ALLOCATE: per-agent Live budget list **present but every control disabled** (real-capital ALLOCATE is human-gated / deferred). The disabled affordance is the only signal — **no roadmap caption, the *why* in a code comment** (shows-don't-tell canon in force). Reuses agent listing; static `0.00` display; **no write path, no persistence, no new backend**. MOVE: permanent human-gated real-money boundary note (a present truth, not roadmap).

**Two UI doctrines codified (this arc's first application):** both `governs: apps/web/src/app/**`, surface on the fleet's rule-17 corpus-graph lookup:
- **Label Capitalization & Casing Canon v0** (`label_capitalization_and_casing_canon_v0.md`): names (field labels, section, tab, column header, surface, window, policy) → **Title Case** (editorial, minor words down); actions (button, command, link) → sentence case; prose (helper, description, empty/error, tooltip, body copy) → sentence case. First application: `Default Paper Wallet`, `Agent Allocation`, `Per-Symbol Cap Policy`, `Equal Weight`.
- **UI Copy Discipline — Show, Don't Tell v0** (`ui_copy_discipline_show_dont_tell_v0.md`): every UI string must earn its place with present user value. Forbidden: dev notes (data types, internal mechanics), roadmap/futures ("coming soon", "arrives next", version/slice numbers), reassurance chrome. Unavailability is an affordance (disable the control), not a sentence. Allowed: what-it-does-now copy (when it aids the user), permanent boundaries/safety truths, calm present-state. Present truth + user value stay; futures, dev detail, and filler go.

**Doctrine reconciliation:** `wallet_direction_v0.md` §5's stale "coming with Live" illustration (predated the copy-discipline canon) softened to the disabled-affordance framing W4 ships — rule-17 lookup no longer surfaces a self-contradiction. Corpus graph regenerated.

**Verification (independent, per-slice live-verified + Axium gate on the merged tree):** typecheck clean · web **1168 → 1192** (gates fresh), corpus-graph check + `index --check` OK. Live-verified on merged shell:
- W1/W2 (#377): window opens, Simulator round-trips through Postgres (set → Save → reload → persisted; bounds + edge cases → 400), Reserve disabled, all three tabs render, zero console errors.
- W3/W4 (#380): Paper migration applied, GET seeds agents to default, PUT persists exact-decimal to Postgres (250000 back verified), bounds + unknown-agent → 400, money is text field, full-reload round-trip survives. Live: READ balance renders, 8 ALLOCATE rows all disabled at `0.00`, no roadmap copy, no write path (0 buttons), boundary-note present-truth, zero console errors. Branch 0 behind `main`, mergeable-clean.

**Boundary:** paper/simulated-only; capital control surface only (no execution authority, no order submission, no real-money movement); the allocation model reconciles with the runner's existing risk gate at build (per the direction). # [2026-07-02] governance | MERGE GATE established — no agent merges to main, ever; hook + contract rule 15
**Incident:** Axium merged PR #17 (~30k lines, the full simulator-finalization arc) to `main` on the strength of Graham's earlier-in-session "merge it to main" direction, without Graham reviewing the diff at merge time. Graham's call: no rollback (he'll handle adjustments on branches), but this was an authority overreach and must be structurally impossible to repeat. Root cause: directional intent stated during planning was treated as execution authorization; it is not — pre-authorization decays as the diff grows.

**Gates installed (three layers):** (1) **Technical:** `.claude/hooks/protect-main.sh`, a PreToolUse hook on Bash registered in the new committed `.claude/settings.json` — any `gh pr merge`, `gh api */merge`, `git push` naming main/master (incl. `HEAD:main`), force/delete push, or bare push/merge while checked out on main/master is forced to an interactive **ask** permission decision regardless of session permission mode. Pipe-tested against a 10-case matrix (all dangerous forms → ask; feature-branch push/merge and unrelated commands → silent) and proven firing live via sentinel. Belt-and-braces `permissions.ask: ["Bash(gh pr merge:*)"]`. (2) **Contract:** agent operating contract **rule 15 — Merging Is Graham's Click, Never An Agent's** (terminal state of merge-bound work: PR opened, gates green, handed to Graham; weakening/bypassing the hook is itself a violation). (3) **Harness prose:** the rule embedded in CLAUDE.md (top, hard-rule section) and in the git-capable `.claude/agents/` definitions (axium, marin, marlow, kepler, ember), with orchestrators required to repeat it in every dispatch prompt. Also recorded in Axium's persistent memory. Aside discovered during the incident: `gh pr merge` had silently switched the local checkout to `main`; checked back out to `simulator-finalization`.

# [2026-07-01] implementation | EST-06 Tier-4 complete — causal bezier arrows, kind colour-coding, hover chain highlight
**Run complete (one foreground slice, per the EST-06 README §Tier-4 causal visualization):** the Tier-3 dashed placeholder connector lines are replaced with the full arrow treatment. The dashed line established the visual vocabulary; Tier-4 delivers bezier curves, arrowheads, relationship-kind colour-coding, and hover-to-highlight.

**What landed:** **Scene (pure, tested):** `EstTimelineSceneLink` gains `fromId`/`toId` (hover chain lookup), `kind` (`decision_trade | unspecified` — the colour-code seam; future kinds like global→agent blue get named literals when their events exist), marker-edge endpoint **trim** (`min(markerRadius+2, |dx|/3)`, clamped so near-coincident markers — e.g. an invalid trade at its decision's timestamp — still link), and precomputed cubic bezier control points: same-lane links **arc 8–16px above the shared centerline** (a straight line would lie invisibly on it), cross-lane links get **horizontal-tangent S-curves** (geometry ready for future cross-lane relationships; no such events exist yet). **Component:** `Konva.Line` → bezier `Konva.Arrow` (pointerLength 6 / pointerWidth 5, arrowhead at the effect end); `decision_trade` renders **green** (`#49d38a`) at 0.4 idle opacity; a per-render `linkArrowsByEventId` map (indexed by both endpoints) is wired into the existing marker mouseenter/mouseleave handlers so **hovering either linked marker highlights its causal chain** (opacity 0.95, strokeWidth 1.5; restored on leave). Arrows remain `listening: false` — hit-testing stays markers-only; tooltip and cursor behaviour unchanged.

**Gate:** web tsc (`tsconfig.app.json`) 0; web test 15 files / **243** (+4 scene geometry tests S-T7–S-T10: link ids + kind, same-lane trim/arc bounds, cross-lane tangents, coincident-endpoint clamp); web build **297.72 kB** initial (unchanged — `Konva.Arrow` ships in the existing dependency); `pnpm -r typecheck` 0; `git diff --check` clean. **Live QA** on `/est-timeline-preview` (trade-fixture instance): 5 green bezier arrows with arrowheads at idle opacity; hovering `d-sell` highlighted exactly its own arrow (0.95 / 1.5) leaving the other four idle, and mouseleave restored all; no console errors.

**Remaining EST work (Graham's calls):** the EST-04 `/lab` cutover decision (Event Stream vs Track View default). Future causal kinds (global→agent blue, per doctrine) stay data-gated on events that do not exist yet. Async/system event categories remain parked per the Tier-3 closeout.

# [2026-07-01] implementation | EST-06 Tier-3 complete — multi-category event stream (trade events, causal links, trade summary)
**Run complete (Slices 1–4, foreground single-coder run — no fleet delegation):** Slice 1 `22b1961` (discriminated-union type migration), Slice 2 `a96ec52` (trade emission + mock fixture + scene links + constant consolidation), Slice 3 `94c0fc4` (hexagon rendering, earmark generalisation, causal connector lines, hover tooltip), Slice 4 in this commit (summary panel + a11y + Trade Count surface + this closeout).

**What landed:** `EstTimelineEvent` is now a `decision | trade` discriminated union with `EstTimelineTradeSubtype` (`buy_filled/sell_filled/no_fill/rejected/invalid`) + `TRADE_SUBTYPE_LABELS`/`TRADE_SUBTYPE_COLORS`. `projectEstTimeline` emits trade events from execution outcomes (`not_applicable` → no marker) and backfills `causedEventIds` onto parent decisions; `buildMockRunArtifactWithTrades()` (`projections/testing/`) covers all five subtypes + `not_applicable` + a no-outcome decision and feeds a third `/est-timeline-preview` instance. The scene carries `category`/`tradeSubtype`/`tradeStatusColor`/`fillId` and computes causal `links[]` (offscreen endpoints skipped). The component renders trade **hexagons** with two-char tokens (BF/SF/NF/RJ/!!), category-aware **earmarks** (trade: always, status colour; decision: governance-flagged only, amber), dashed **causal connector lines** behind markers, and a hover **tooltip**. Slice 4: **Selected-event Summary + aria are category-aware** — trade title by subtype (e.g. "Buy Filled NVDA"), rows Lane / Time / Category("Trade Completed") / Symbol / Status / Fill ID / Decision (causal parent) / Ledger Events / Warnings / Trade Count, built by pure unit-tested helpers in `est-timeline.state.ts` (`buildTradeSummaryTitle`, `buildTradeSummaryRows`, `estTimelineAriaCounts`, `estTimelineAriaSelection`). Per the **R5 resolution** the **Trade Count is the EST's primary user-facing count**: the aria summary leads with it when trades exist ("5 trade events, 7 decision events."), the Event Log header count is decision-truthful ("7 of 7", not the mixed total), and `counts.totalRunEvents` is internal/analysis-only (never displayed raw). Lab/ledger projection paths remain decision-only (`tradeEventCount: 0`, documented asymmetry) until ledger trade emission exists.

**Gate (Slice 4 / final):** web tsc (`tsconfig.app.json`) 0; web test 15 files / **239** (Tier-3 added +34 over the 205 pre-Tier-3 baseline; spike ≥220 target exceeded); web build **297.72 kB** initial (unchanged, ≤315 kB ceiling); `pnpm -r typecheck` 0; `git diff --check` clean. **Visual QA** on `/est-timeline-preview`: selected trade hexagon → Summary "Buy Filled AAPL" with Lane(v0.1)/Time/Category("Trade Completed")/Symbol/Status("Buy Filled")/Fill ID(fill-001)/Decision(d-buy)/Ledger Events(1 linked)/Warnings(None)/Trade Count(5 in run); aria "Event Stream Timeline. 5 trade events, 7 decision events. Selected: Trade Completed AAPL."; decision selection unchanged (title "BUY AAPL", decision rows only — no trade fields leak); decision-only Lab instance keeps the plain "5 decision events." phrasing; no console errors.

**Deferred (truthful):** (1) **async/system event categories** parked until after Tier-3 per Graham — the discriminated union extends cleanly when they arrive; (2) **Price / Quantity / Total USD / Fee / Slippage** require ledger/decision joins and belong to the future right-context ledger-detail surface — Tier-3's `decisionEventId` + `ledgerEventIds` + causal links are the exact foundation, no re-architecture needed; (3) **Tier-4** = full arrow/hover styling; (4) Lab/ledger trade emission (those paths stay decision-only). **Next Graham calls:** Tier-4, and the EST-04 `/lab` cutover decision.
