# [2026-07-01] implementation | EST-04 Slice 3c — bidirectional selection sync for the EST timeline
Closed the bidirectional-selection follow-up from the `/lab` mount. Problem: `EstTimelineComponent`'s sticky interaction override (`selectionOverride`, set on an in-EST canvas/log click, taking precedence via `effectiveSelection = selectionOverride() ?? selection()`) masked later changes to the external `selection` input — so a run picked in the Workbench wouldn't reflect in the EST view after the user had clicked inside it. Fix: a constructor `effect` watches the external `selection()` input and, when its `selectedEventId` changes to a different value (tracked via a plain `lastExternalSelectionId` field), clears the override so the external selection wins. It reads the input signal and writes a *different* signal (the override) → no self-loop; where nothing wires selection back (the Simulator / `/est-timeline-preview`), the input never changes and the override persists, preserving today's behavior. Verified on `/est-timeline-preview` (added an "External select (simulate Workbench)" control + made the harness selection a signal): initial external d3 (S:AAPL) → in-EST click wins (d1, B:AAPL) → external change wins over the sticky override (d6, S:AMD) → in-EST click still works afterward (NVDA HOLD); no console errors. Gate: web typecheck 0, `15` files / `196` tests, build OK (initial `297.68 kB` unchanged), `pnpm -r typecheck` 0, `git diff --check` clean. Committed `4c5164f`. Remaining EST-04 follow-up: populated in-`/lab` visual QA needs the API + active agent. Next: Slice 4 — parity validation vs the Track View, then a separate explicit Graham cutover decision.

# [2026-07-01] implementation | EST-04 Slice 3 — EST timeline mounted in /lab (opt-in, alongside the Track View)
Design finding at the mount: `TimelineWorkspaceComponent` (the main `/lab` timeline) already receives the *already-filtered* `LabV2TimelineEvent[]` + `tracks` and emits `eventSelect`, so the mount should consume that pre-filtered set rather than re-project from the raw ledger — resolving seams #7 (reuse Lab filters), #5 (reuse fetched-evidence window), #4 (reuse track order as lanes), and #6 (keep the LabV2 event id so selection needs no translation) at once, entirely local to the component (no OperatorConsole/store change). **3a** (`c5c519d`): pure `projectEstTimelineFromLabV2Events(events, tracks, agentName?)` — a new `est-timeline.from-lab-v2.projection.ts` adapter producing the shared `EstTimelineModel` from the Lab Track-View evidence: lanes from tracks (+ bottom Global/Source), token via the reused `labActionToDecision`, governance summary mapped from the LabV2 governance fields (seam #3), time/symbols/counts, `lab_evidence_fetched_window_only` boundary. `projectEstTimelineFromLedger` (Slices 1–2) is retained as the lower-level raw-ledger path (proved the derivations). +5-test spec; pure/unwired. **3b** (`b06bd3c`): mounted it in `TimelineWorkspaceComponent` — a local `renderMode` toggle (Track View | Event Stream, default `classic` = opt-in, no cutover), an `estModel` computed adapting the component's own `filteredEvents()` + `tracks()`, an `estSelection` from the existing `selectedEventId`/`selectedTrackId` inputs, and `onEstEventSelected` routing EST's `eventSelected` (id = LabV2 event id) straight into the existing `eventSelect` output → OperatorStore decision↔timeline sync (no id translation). Bottom Workbench / Right Context keep the full governance detail; the timeline carries the spine + attention marker. Verified live on `/lab` (isolated 4310 server, no API): `↩ Lab` → Track View shows the toggle; Event Stream activates and mounts `<app-est-timeline>`, rendering the clean "No decision evidence in the current window." empty state without error (real evidence needs the API + active agent, as with EST-03 4c); the populated render path is proven via the `/est-timeline-preview` Lab-fed instance + the 3a unit tests; no console errors; screenshot captured. Gate (both sub-slices): web typecheck 0, `15` files / `196` tests, build OK (initial `297.68 kB` unchanged), `pnpm -r typecheck` 0, `git diff --check` clean. **Follow-ups: populated in-`/lab` visual QA needs the API + active agent; bidirectional selection hardening (an external selection change after an in-EST click is masked by the component's selection override). Next: Slice 4 — parity validation vs the Track View, then a separate explicit Graham cutover decision.**

# [2026-07-01] implementation | EST-04 Slice 2 — governance attention marker (spine + marker)
Graham chose EST-04 decision #3 = **spine + attention marker** (keep EST the causal spine; one at-a-glance attention signal on the event; full detail routes to the Workbench). Implemented in two sub-slices. **2a data** (`2208e61`): extended the shared, environment-agnostic `EstTimelineModel` with an optional `governance` summary on the event — `flagged`, `reasons` (`warning` | `follow_up` | `replay_candidate`), `reviewState`, `replayDisposition`, `warningCount`, `annotationCount` (not color-only; reasons are text tokens). The Lab (run-ledger) projection populates it via `toGovernance()` — flagged when warningCount>0, unresolved follow-up, or replay candidate (mirrors the Lab Track View `attention` priority); the Simulator path leaves it undefined. The scene surfaces `flagged` on the event block. **2b render** (`ca3579f`): a non-color-only top-right corner **wedge** on flagged canvas blocks (shape encodes attention, dims when offscreen), a compact "⚠ <reason> +N" Event Log badge with a full "Needs attention: …" accessible title (⚠ glyph aria-hidden; reason text carries meaning), and an "N need attention" clause appended to the canvas `ariaSummary`. The `/est-timeline-preview` harness gained a second Lab-fed instance (`projectEstTimelineFromLedger` over a run-ledger fixture with flagged runs) for QA. Verified live on `/est-timeline-preview`: 3 flagged runs render wedge + badge with correct reasons, token derivation correct (NO_TRADE→A), aria reads "5 decision events. 3 need attention."; no console errors; screenshot captured. Full review/replay/annotation detail still routes to the Workbench on selection (Slice 3). Gate (both sub-slices): web typecheck 0, `14` files / `191` tests, build OK (initial `297.68 kB` unchanged), `pnpm -r typecheck` 0, `git diff --check` clean. **Next: Slice 3 — mount into `/lab` alongside the preserved Track View, wiring `eventSelected` into the existing OperatorStore decision↔timeline sync (seams #5/#6/#7).**

# [2026-07-01] implementation | EST-04 Slice 1 — pure Lab-ledger → EstTimelineModel projection
Started EST-04 (Graham's go). Since the EST renderer is source-agnostic (renders an `EstTimelineModel`), making it the main `/lab` timeline is a second projection, not a component rewrite. Added `apps/web/src/app/projections/est-timeline.from-ledger.projection.ts`: pure `projectEstTimelineFromLedger(RunLedgerEntry[], AgentArtifact, AgentVersion[]) → EstTimelineModel` producing the same model `projectEstTimeline` builds from a Simulator run. Slice 1 = the causal spine only: seam #1 token derivation (`labActionToDecision` maps the ledger action string onto B/A/S/H — BUY→B, SELL→S, HOLD→H, and `ABSTAIN`/`NO_TRADE`/empty/unknown→A, since the calibration gate emits `NO_TRADE` and the ledger has no token; exported + unit-tested); per-version lanes (Versioned Agent tracks) ordered active-first → latest activity → label (decision #4) with a bottom `Global / Source` lane and an `unversioned` fallback lane; `execution: null` (Lab runs have no simulated fills) and `warnings: []` (the Lab's governance/review metadata is decision #3, deferred to Slice 2); symbols uppercased/sorted (symbol-less events still render), deterministic time range, truthful counts, `boundaryLabels: ['lab_evidence_fetched_window_only']` to preserve the fetched-window honesty. Pure, unwired — no component/renderer/store/data-fetch, no consumer yet; initial bundle unchanged `297.68 kB`; +10 tests. Gate: web typecheck 0, `14` files / `189` tests, build OK, `pnpm -r typecheck` 0, `git diff --check` clean. Committed `6bd5e36`. **Next: checkpoint with Graham on decision #3 (governance-vs-causal metadata) before Slice 2.** The existing `LabV2` Track View is preserved.

# [2026-07-01] design spike | EST-04 Lab Timeline Convergence Spike authored
With EST-03 4e complete, Graham chose to scope the convergence question (whether the EST timeline also becomes the main `/lab` non-simulator timeline) rather than keep EST simulator-only. Authored the docs-only spike packet at `docs/design/v2/timeline/event-stream-timeline-doctrine-v0/est-04-lab-timeline-convergence-spike/README.md` and indexed it (context index + doctrine README nav). Grounded in implementation truth: the EST renderer stack (`est-timeline.component/.scene/.state`) renders an `EstTimelineModel` and is **source-agnostic**, so convergence is a *second pure projection* (`projectEstTimelineFromLedger(RunLedgerEntry[], AgentArtifact, AgentVersion[]) → EstTimelineModel`), not a component rewrite; and the existing `lab-v2-timeline.projection.ts` already builds per-version **tracks** (= EST Versioned Agent lanes), filters, fetched-evidence time windows, and run selection. The packet lays the two sources side by side and surfaces 7 seams/decisions: (1) B/A/S/H **token derivation** from the Lab's `action` string incl. calibration-gate `NO_TRADE→A` (Lab has no token); (2) null execution subrecord for Lab (no simulated fills — already renders); (3) **the crux — governance-vs-causal metadata**: the Lab timeline's core value is review/replay/annotation/warning state; decide whether to extend the EST event with a governance subrecord (a) or keep EST the causal spine and route governance to the Workbench via existing selection sync (b, recommended for the first cut); (4) lane reconciliation + empty global/source lane; (5) time model — EST 4e viewport vs Lab fetched-evidence presets (preserve the `isBoundedToFetchedEvidence` honesty); (6) selection routing — wire EST's `eventSelected` into the existing OperatorStore decision↔timeline sync closures via runId; (7) filter reconciliation — recommend consuming the pre-filtered Lab set so the richer Lab filter UI is preserved. Sequenced foundation-first (Slice 1 pure projection + spec → Slice 2 governance posture → Slice 3 `/lab` mount alongside Track View → Slice 4 parity + explicit Graham cutover). Boundary: docs-only; **preserve the existing `LabV2` Track View until parity is proven and Graham approves cutover** — convergence must not silently drop the Lab's governance/review surface. No source/renderer/store/API/dependency change; no live/broker/P&L behavior. Committed with the packet. Not yet activated for implementation — Slice 1 awaits Graham's go + his steer on decision #3.

# [2026-07-01] implementation | EST-03 Slice 4e-4 — Timeline a11y / text-crispness / reduced-motion polish (4e leg complete)
Closed the Slice 4e transport+filter+a11y leg with accessibility and rendering polish against the EST-02 acceptance criteria. (1) Rich accessible name for the canvas (`role=img`): an `ariaSummary` computed gives a screen reader the timeline's state — total events, active symbol filter + shown count, viewport count, and the current selection, including ", hidden by the current filter" when filtered out (verified: default "Event Stream Timeline. 6 decision events. Selected: SELL AAPL." → NVDA filter "…Filtered to NVDA: 2 shown. Selected: SELL AAPL, hidden by the current filter."). (2) Text-crispness: cap Konva `pixelRatio` at 2 — crisp HiDPI text without over-allocating the backing store on >2x displays. (3) Reduced-motion: subtle 0.12s hover transitions on the transport/filter/log controls, disabled under `prefers-reduced-motion`; viewport/zoom/filter changes are already instant (no tweens), so the surface carries no essential motion. Non-color-only encoding preserved throughout (canvas B/A/S/H token text; filter chips carry the symbol label beside the color dot; log rows fully textual). Verified live on `/est-timeline-preview` (aria-label updates across states, no console errors, screenshot captured). Gate: web typecheck 0, `13` files / `179` tests, build OK (initial `297.68 kB` unchanged), `pnpm -r typecheck` 0, `git diff --check` clean. Committed `afa3a91`. **The EST-03 Slice 4e leg is complete.** Open question now surfaced for Graham (not assumed): whether the EST timeline also replaces the main Lab (non-simulator) timeline surface — its own decision, not folded into 4e.

# [2026-07-01] implementation | EST-03 Slice 4e-3 — Timeline symbol filter UI (All-semantics)
Added the symbol filter UI over the already-tested `filterEstTimelineEvents`, wired through the same component-internal-signal seam as selection/transport (so the simulator mount and `/est-timeline-preview` harness stay untouched). Component-owned `filtersOverride` signal falls back to the `filters` input; `effectiveFilters` feeds `resolveEstTimelineViewState`, so the canvas, Event Log, and the visible-in-log/total counts all move together (structural sync). A chip row (shown when >1 symbol): `All` + one per run symbol, each with the shared symbol-color dot (`assignSymbolColors` — same source as the canvas blocks and Symbol Graph), real buttons with `aria-pressed`. `All`-semantics: from `All` the first click narrows to that symbol; removing the last active symbol falls back to `All` rather than an empty timeline; multi-select otherwise. Preserve-detail (EST-02 open decision #7): when the current selection is filtered out, a `role=status` note ("Selected event is hidden by the current filter.") surfaces it rather than silently clearing. Also deduped `render()`'s `assignSymbolColors` onto a shared `symbolColorMap` computed. Verified live on `/est-timeline-preview`: All active by default (6 of 6); clicking NVDA → NVDA-only canvas + log (2 of 6), All deselected, and the pre-selected AAPL event trips the filtered-out note; no console errors; screenshot captured. Gate: web typecheck 0, `13` files / `179` tests, build OK (initial `297.68 kB` unchanged), `pnpm -r typecheck` 0, `git diff --check` clean. Committed `899a96b`. Next (4e-4): a11y / text-crispness / reduced-motion polish.

# [2026-07-01] implementation | EST-03 Slice 4e-2 — Timeline transport (Fit / Zoom / Follow-now)
Wired the Slice 4e-1 viewport math into `EstTimelineComponent`, keeping Angular the state owner and Konva a thin painter. Added component-internal `viewport` and `followNow` signals (mirroring the existing `selectionOverride` seam, so the simulator mount and `/est-timeline-preview` harness stay untouched; default = Fit = the prior full-run render). A transport control row above the canvas — Fit / Zoom− / Zoom+ / Follow-now as real buttons with aria-labels, Fit + Follow-now reflecting state via `is-active`/`aria-pressed`. The active window feeds BOTH `buildEstTimelineScene(timeRange)` (so the canvas scales over the zoomed window, dimming/clamping offscreen blocks) and `resolveEstTimelineViewState(…, viewport)`, surfacing the now-truthful **rendered-in-viewport** count ("N in view") distinct from visible-in-log and total. Follow-now re-pins to the run's latest edge on each zoom; Fit clears the window; selecting an Event Log row focuses the viewport via `viewportForEvent` but only when already zoomed in (at Fit the whole run is already visible). Verified live on `/est-timeline-preview` via the preview MCP: Fit default (no count), two zoom-ins → "1 in view" (single centered event), Follow-now → `aria-pressed=true` + "2 in view" (latest-edge window), Fit → reset; no console errors; screenshot captured. Gate: web typecheck 0, `13` files / `179` tests, build OK (initial `297.68 kB` unchanged), `pnpm -r typecheck` 0, `git diff --check` clean. Committed `d7198ba`. Next (4e-3): symbol filter UI over `filterEstTimelineEvents`.

# [2026-07-01] implementation | EST-03 Slice 4e-1 — Timeline viewport transport math (pure)
Opened the Slice 4e transport+filter leg with its Angular-owned foundation: pure viewport math in `apps/web/src/app/projections/est-timeline.state.ts` that owns the "which window" decision so the 4e-2 component stays a thin renderer over tested geometry. Added `fullRangeViewport(model)` (the whole run as a window — Fit), `zoomEstTimelineViewport(fullRange, current, factor, {anchor, minSpanMs})` (scales the span; `<1` zooms in, `>1` out; floored at `EST_TIMELINE_MIN_VIEWPORT_SPAN_MS` = 60s so time never compresses to a strip, capped at the full range; re-anchored on the window center or a fixed ms; clamped), and `followNowViewport(fullRange, spanMs)` (a right-anchored window pinned to the run's latest edge — a viewport posture, not reversed time, per EST-02 acceptance). Factored the edge-preserving clamp out of `viewportForEvent` into a shared `clampWindowToRange` (windows shift inward at boundaries rather than shrink); `viewportForEvent`'s existing spec still guards it. The scene already scales over any `timeRange` window, so no scene/renderer change was needed here. Added 10 transport tests. Pure, unwired — no component, renderer, Konva, dependency, or API change; initial bundle unchanged at `297.68 kB`. Gate: web typecheck 0, `13` files / `179` tests, build OK, `pnpm -r typecheck` 0, `git diff --check` clean. Committed `781a858`. Next (4e-2): drive this math from `EstTimelineComponent` (Fit / Zoom± / Follow-now controls, windowed scene + truthful rendered-in-viewport count, row-click focus).

# [2026-06-30] implementation | EST-03 Slice 4d — interactive Timeline + synced Event Log
Made the Slice-2 structural sync visible and interactive. The Konva canvas now hit-tests decision blocks (each block carries an `estEventId` attr; one stage `click`/`tap` handler reads it): clicking a block selects the event + its lane via `selectEstTimelineEvent` (lighting the selected ring), clicking empty canvas clears. Added a DOM **Event Log** companion inside `EstTimelineComponent` — a `role=listbox` of the same shared filtered set with keyboard-focusable `role=option` rows (token, ET time, version, symbol, action, execution status) that drive the identical selection, with the selected row highlighted in lockstep with the canvas ring. One selection signal (`selectionOverride`, falling back to the `selection` input) feeds both surfaces, so canvas and log cannot drift; the log header shows the truthful "visible-in-log of total-run" count. Selection updates run through `NgZone.run` so DOM change detection stays in sync with Konva's canvas events. Verified live on `/est-timeline-preview` (fixture data): canvas + Event Log rendered, and clicking the AAPL/BUY log row moved both the row highlight and the canvas selection ring to that event. Gate: web typecheck 0, `13` files / `169` tests, build OK (initial `297.68 kB` unchanged), `git diff --check` clean. Committed `6bc7dbb`. Next (4e+): follow-now/zoom, filter UI, screen-reader polish.

# [2026-06-30] implementation | EST-03 Slice 4c mount — Timeline in the Simulator center
Mounted the EST timeline into the center simulation space (where Symbol/Version Graph live), per Graham's decision to repurpose the Simulator 'Timeline' view. The `simulator-workspace` already held the loaded run as a `scriptedRunArtifact` signal (the same one Version Graph projects), so `estTimelineModel = projectEstTimeline(scriptedRunArtifact())` drops in with no new data plumbing. The 'Timeline' button now does `selectView('timeline')` and renders `<app-est-timeline>` in-place beside Symbol Graph and Version Graph, instead of emitting `returnToTimeline` (which exited to the Lab timeline). A new '↩ Lab' control preserves that exit (still emits `returnToTimeline` → parent `modeChange.emit('timeline')`). The timeline view shows 'All symbols' in the symbol selector and an empty-state card ('Generate a scripted fixture run…') when there is no run. Verified live on an isolated web dev server: the Timeline view activates in-place (no longer exits), the ↩ Lab exit is present, and the pre-load empty state is correct; the populated canvas render was already screenshot-verified via `/est-timeline-preview` (same component + model). Full in-simulator populated QA needs the API running for run generation. No interaction/hit-testing yet (Slice 4d). Gate: web typecheck 0, `13` files / `169` tests (workspace spec green), build OK (initial `297.68 kB` unchanged), `git diff --check` clean. Committed `e3194d1`.

# [2026-06-30] implementation | EST-03 Slice 4b — Konva Timeline renderer (draw-only) + visual QA
Graham reconciled the pnpm store (fixing the v10/v11 mismatch), unblocking the dependency add. Added `konva ^10.3.0` to `apps/web` — isolated in a lazy route chunk, so the initial bundle only moved `297.45 → 297.68 kB`. Built `EstTimelineComponent` (`apps/web/src/app/workstation/features/timeline/est-timeline.component.ts`) as a thin draw-only renderer over the pure Slice 4a scene geometry: it resolves the view state, builds the scene at the measured container width, and paints lane rows + B/A/S/H decision blocks onto a Konva canvas, colored by the now-exported shared Simulator symbol source (`assignSymbolColors`). Angular still owns all state; Konva only paints. Browser-only, ResizeObserver-driven, cleaned up on destroy, with a truthful-count aria-label. A self-contained `/est-timeline-preview` route renders it over a synthetic fixture. Visual QA: served a second dev server (port 4310, since 4200 was in use), screenshotted `/est-timeline-preview`, and confirmed two Versioned Agent lanes (v0.1, v0.2) + bottom Global/Source lane, B/A/S/H blocks positioned left-to-right by time with per-symbol colors consistent across lanes and the selected event's cyan ring. Found and fixed one clip bug (event at the max timestamp overhung the right edge) by insetting the scene axis by half a block in `buildEstTimelineScene`; re-screenshotted clean. No interaction/tooltips/follow-now yet, and not wired to live persisted-run data yet (later sub-slices). Gate each step: web typecheck 0, `13` files / `169` tests, build OK (konva lazy-chunked), `pnpm -r typecheck` 0, `git diff --check` clean. Committed `05e9422` (component + preview) and `08136b3` (clip fix).

# [2026-06-30] implementation | EST-03 Slice 2 — Timeline selection/filter/Event-Log-sync state
Implemented EST-03 Slice 2: `apps/web/src/app/projections/est-timeline.state.ts` — a pure, unwired view-state seam over the Slice 1 projection. The Timeline scene and the Event Log render the same filtered event set and share one selection, so sync is structural (`eventLogRows === filteredEvents`; `selectEstTimelineEvent` is the single selection point for both surfaces). The three event counts stay distinct and un-conflated: `totalRunEvents` vs `visibleInLog` (filtered) vs `renderedInViewport` (filtered within the viewport window). `resolveEstTimelineViewState` derives the view; `viewportForEvent` computes a range-clamped pan/focus window for the "select a log row → focus the Timeline" behavior; `clearEstTimelineSelection` resets. Resolved EST-02 open decision #7 as **preserve-detail**: a filtered-out selection is kept with a `filtered_out` indicator rather than cleared by rule (flippable in one place). Added a 13-test characterization spec. Still no renderer, Konva, Angular store, dependency, or data fetch; bundle `297.45 kB` unchanged. Gate: web typecheck 0, `12` files / `163` tests, build OK, `pnpm -r typecheck` 0, `git diff --check` clean. Committed `28dd361`. Next: Slice 3 (persisted-run web read path) then Slice 4+ (Konva renderer, separate Graham-approved dependency-gated slice).

# [2026-06-30] implementation | OperatorStore decomposed into composed signalStoreFeature files
Completed the behavior-preserving decomposition of `apps/web/src/app/core/state/operator.store.ts` (the ~1,970-line `@ngrx/signals` `signalStore`) into focused `signalStoreFeature` files, behind the 138-test characterization guardrail. Graham chose feature composition into the existing `OperatorStore` (not separate injectable stores), so the public surface stayed byte-identical, the 4 consumer components were untouched, and the guardrail suite ran unchanged. `operator.store.ts` is now a 39-line assembler composing ten features in dependency order; the logic lives in `features/core.feature.ts` (foundational computeds + core commands), `features/agent.feature.ts` (version-analysis computeds + agent commands), `features/lab-timeline.feature.ts` (timeline/governance computeds + selection-sync engine + timeline commands), `features/simulator.feature.ts`, and `features/operator-feature.util.ts` (shared `formatApiError`). Committed in five slices: `09dff04` simulator, `4433667` harness hardening, `3a7dc12` computeds (core/agent/lab-timeline), `3231a47` timeline-sync + core-command methods, `8275e93` agent + lab-timeline commands + assembler collapse. The dual decision<->timeline filter path was consolidated into two private closures (`applyTimelineFilters` / `applyDecisionFilters`) preserving the review-filter (no decision mirror) and symbol-query (`runIds`-clear) exceptions and the bespoke `applyReplayTarget`. Two `@ngrx/signals` composition rules were learned and encoded in the coder harness (cross-feature calls use `store.` not `this.`; a typed feature's inputs must come from earlier features/state, never a remaining inline block, or the store type collapses to an index signature) — the latter drove a corrected bottom-up slice order after a first Agent-slice attempt failed and was reverted. Each slice passed the full gate (web typecheck, 138/138 tests, build 297.45 kB unchanged, `pnpm -r typecheck`, `git diff --check`). No product behavior, package/lock, or API/backend change. This clears the OperatorStore-legibility item; next in the roadmap is EST-03.

# [2026-06-30] tooling | Hardened Claude agent harnesses for reliable phase-gated runs

Additive edits only; no application source, package files, or runtime behavior changed.

# [2026-06-30] infrastructure + implementation | DB migration runner and Historic Simulator run persistence
Implemented two foundation packets on `simulator-finalization`, verified by running the API against the local Postgres container, with full-workspace `corepack pnpm -r typecheck` passing. This is the first implementation work in the Codex→Claude development transition; the agreed working model is foundation-first, incremental/test-guarded, lightweight checkpoints.

Packet 1 — DB migration runner: replaced the embedded `MIGRATION_SQL` blob in `apps/api/src/services/database.service.ts` with an ordered, transactional, idempotent file-based runner (`apps/api/src/services/migration-runner.ts`) tracked by a new `schema_versions` table. Reconciled `infra/migrations/001_init.sql` to current runtime truth (dropped the orphaned `trade_orders` table that runtime never created; folded in the defensive column backfills). `ensureSchema()` keeps its `user_settings` seed and stale-`running`-run cleanup. Safe on both fresh databases and legacy databases created by the old embedded blob (idempotent DDL recorded/baselined). No product behavior changed.

Packet 2 — Historic Simulator run persistence: supersedes the prior `in_memory_only_no_run_persistence` Simulator boundary (see ADR-006). Added `infra/migrations/002_simulator_run_persistence.sql` (`simulator_runs` storing the full artifact JSON plus promoted index columns, and `simulator_run_trigger_events` storing the schedule-interrupting decision records), `apps/api/src/services/historic-simulator/simulator-run-persistence.service.ts` (idempotent upsert + trigger-event replace, list, get-by-id), wired persistence into `runScriptedScenario`, added `GET /simulator/runs` and `GET /simulator/runs/:runId`, and registered the service in `app.module.ts`. Persistence boundary decided with Graham: persist the run record + per-branch decision/valuation summary plus only the triggering (decision-evaluation) events that interrupt the runner's normal schedule; the regenerable interpolated MicroPath stream is NOT persisted. Verified round-trip: the synthetic demo scripted run persisted 1 run + 7 triggering events, re-run was idempotent (still 1 run / 7 events), and `schema_versions` recorded `001` and `002`.

Also added the Claude-side agent harness mechanism mirroring `.codex/agents/`: `.claude/agents/rin-librarian.md` ports the Context Librarian role (model `haiku`), plus `.claude/agents/README.md` documenting the port plan and status. Recorded `ADR-006` for the persistence-boundary change, added contradiction-register tension #15 (in-artifact `persistenceBoundary` literal vs the real DB persistence posture), and updated `index.md`, `CURRENT_STATE.md`, and `current_priorities.md`.

No web/app source, package manifests, lockfiles, provider calls, Agent execution, Paper Flow, Live Paper, LIVE, broker/order/fill/true-P&L behavior, or professional conclusions were introduced. Deferred follow-ups: reconcile the shared `SimulatorScriptedRunArtifact` `persistenceBoundary` literal when web first consumes persisted runs (contradiction #15); build the web read path for persisted runs.
